Abstract: We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.

</details>


### [141] [Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27215)
*Manjinder Singh,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Mohamed Elawady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 GAversary 的黑盒对抗攻击遗传算法，利用 GloVe 嵌入提升语义相似度，在 NLP 任务中表现出比 BAE 和 A2T 更强的攻击性能，显著降低了目标模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 NLP 模型容易受到对抗性攻击（如小的语义保持改动），现有攻击方法要么依赖模型内部结构（白盒），要么在黑盒设置下攻击效果有限，需要更有效的黑盒攻击手段来评估模型鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 GAversary 算法，这是一种混合遗传算法（GA）。该方法仅需目标模型的 Logit 输出（黑盒设置），并利用 GloVe 词嵌入作为变异算子来生成语义上较为相似的词替换以进行攻击。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，GAversary 能够显著降低模型准确率（例如将 76.8% 的准确率降至 5.8%），优于对比方法 BAE（27.6%）和 A2T。其代价是扰动词数略多，语义相似度稍有下降，且运行时间增加约 5%。

Conclusion: GAversary 是一种高效的黑盒对抗攻击方法，能够显著降低 NLP 模型的准确率，虽然在扰动词数和语义相似度上存在权衡，但其攻击强度优于现有的 BAE 和 A2T 方法。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.

</details>


### [142] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BINEVAL 是一种通过将复杂评估任务拆解为细粒度二进制问题来评分的框架，实现了评估结果的高可解释性，并在性能与诊断功能上均超越现有 LLM 评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的 LLM 输出评估存在瓶颈：人工评估成本高且缓慢；词汇级指标（如 BLEU/ROUGE）与人类判断的相关性差； holistic LLM Judge（整体式大模型评估器）评估过程“黑盒”，难以诊断和调试。

Method: 提出 BINEVAL 框架：将评估标准拆解为原子级的二进制问题，利用大模型对这些问题进行独立判定，并将结果汇总生成可解译的多维度评分。同时支持基于这些反馈进行 prompt 的自我优化或跨模型优化。

Result: 在 SummEval、Topical-Chat 和 QAGS 等基准测试中，BINEVAL 表现优于或持平现有的 UniEval 和 G-Eval；特别是在事实一致性方面有显著改进；能够有效规避量化评估中的“天花板效应”，并成功应用于 prompt 的迭代优化。

Conclusion: BINEVAL 是一种无需训练、任务通用的评估框架，不仅在人类判断相关性上表现出色，还提供了可解释的原子级反馈，支持 prompt 的迭代优化，解决了现有 LLM 评估器不透明度过高和评估标准模糊的问题。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [143] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对地球观测预测中的不确定性和气象响应建模问题，提出了一种基于视频扩散Transformer的EO-WM模型，通过整合物理约束条件与新设计的诊断基准，显著增强了模型对极端天气下地表动态（如植被退化）的物理一致性预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测预测模型存在两方面问题：一是确定性模型无法处理预测的不确定性，而现有扩散模型未能有效区分气象强迫信号；二是现有基准过于侧重重构精度，忽略了模型在不同气象强迫下的动态响应能力和物理一致性。

Method: 提出一种基于视频扩散Transformer（Video Diffusion Transformer）的EO-WM架构，引入分层物理信息感知条件（Physically Informed Conditioning）框架：将气象强迫分解为气候基线、天气异常和累积物理压力（如热浪/干旱累积效应）信号，并采用独立的条件路径进行处理。同时构建了两个新的诊断基准：极端夏季基准（Extreme Summer Benchmark）和季节性匹配基准（Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark）。

Result: 在NDVI下降幅度预测中，误差降低了5.63%，方向命中率提高了7.80%；在极端天气响应和季节性变化模拟能力上显著优于现有主流模型，且在传统像素级评价指标上保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: EO-WM模型在处理气象驱动的地球观测预测任务中表现优异。通过引入物理特征感知和分层条件建模，该模型不仅在传统指标上保持竞争优势，更在极端天气响应和季节性变化预测方面显著提升了准确性，为地球科学领域提供了一个更具解释性和可靠性的预测框架。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


### [144] [Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27286)
*Alina Bazarova,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Henrik Zunker,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Torben Heinsohn,Wolfgang Wiechert,Katharina Noeh,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究表明，基于模拟的推断（SBI）在校准传染病模型时，能提供与传统MCMC方法相当的精度，同时将计算时间缩短数十倍至数百倍，显著提升了应对大规模爆发性疫情的实时决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的贝叶斯校准方法（如MCMC）在处理高维非线性传染病模型或进行重复的近实时分析时，计算成本过高，限制了其在公共卫生决策中的应用。因此，需要寻找更具扩展性的替代方案。

Method: 利用神经后验估计（Neural Posterior Estimation）进行基于模拟的推断（SBI），并将其与传统的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法在机械性SECIR流行病学模型（基于德国COVID-19 ICU数据）的校准上进行对比评估。评估涵盖了短周期（31天）和长周期（201天）窗口。

Result: SBI在31天和201天的推断窗口下，均能产生与MCMC高度一致的后验分布及预测结果。同时，SBI展现出巨大的计算效率优势：在31天任务中将耗时从约1000秒缩减至70秒左右，在201天任务中将耗时从19000秒以上缩减至约157秒。

Conclusion: 基于模拟的推断（SBI）是一种快速、高效的贝叶斯校准框架，能显著缩短计算时间，特别适合于需要近实时决策的传染病建模场景。

Abstract: Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.

</details>


### [145] [Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27287)
*Preet Baxi,Jiannan Xu,Jane Yi Jiang,Stefanus Jasin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对LLM驱动的简历筛选系统的提示词注入攻击，发现此类攻击在特定场景下能有效操纵排名，但其效能受限于操控参与度，并对招聘公平性构成潜在威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）被广泛应用于招聘筛选流程，候选人存在利用提示词注入等手段操纵评价结果以提升排名的动机，需要研究此类对抗性攻击的有效性、局限性及其对招聘公平性的影响。

Method: 通过受控实验评估“提示词注入”（Prompt Injection）对自动化简历筛选的影响，具体表现为在简历中加入不含实际资质的自我推销文字，以此考察其对LLM排序结果的影响。

Result: 1. 在简历质量同质化且操控行为稀少时，提示词注入能可靠提升排名；2. 随着采用操控手段的候选人增加，其有效性迅速衰减直至失效；3. 在候选人质量异质化的情况下，注入手段整体效果下降，但仍可能导致严重的公平性问题（低质量者超越高质量者）。

Conclusion: 基于大模型的简历筛选系统在候选人操控行为稀少且能力差异较小时最为脆弱。随着操控行为的普及，其有效性会迅速下降，并可能导致低质量候选人获得不公平的不正当竞争优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26

</details>


### [146] [When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模型LLM系统的提升上限受到“全体错误率”的严格限制。研究表明，现有的模型评估方法普遍低估了所有模型同时出错的概率（beta），盲目增加模型数量的回报极低，性能提升的关键在于模型间的差异性，而非规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模型LLM系统（如路由、投票、级联等）旨在超越单一模型性能，但缺乏对这些系统性能上限的清晰界定，且常规指标（如配对相关性）难以识别系统的实际性能瓶颈。

Method: 通过分析67个模型在数学、编程及GPQA-Diamond数据集上的表现，利用Tetrachoric校准的单因子模型及高斯Copula方法评估“全体错误率”（beta），并对比分析了平均配对错误相关性（rho）与beta的区别。

Result: 发现现有系统对“全体错误率”（beta）存在严重低估（约2.5倍）；在可验证任务中，多模型组合往往难以超越单一最强模型，除非拥有强大的查询级路由信号。

Conclusion: 多模型系统的性能提升受限于“全体错误率”（beta），且现有模型往往低估了这一指标。即便是在低相关性的模型组合中，盲目堆砌模型收效甚微，性能提升的核心在于模型在不同问题上的分布差异，而非模型数量的增加。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router.
  Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.

</details>


### [147] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用LLM构建的老年人语言数字孪生框架，通过多头cVAE验证，证明了该方法在认知健康监测中具有高保真度和预测潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对轻度认知障碍（MCI）早期检测困难的问题，利用语言和对话模式作为非侵入性生物标志物，构建个性化的数字孪生模型进行认知健康监测。

Method: 提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的数字孪生框架，通过整合文体特征（stylometric cues）和上下文元数据来模拟个体对话行为；并引入多头条件变分自编码器（multi-head cVAE）评估数字孪生的保真度与认知一致性。

Result: 在I-CONECT数据集上的实验表明，该模型在身份特征保持、重构质量及MoCA认知评分预测方面表现优异，且优于标准的GPT基准模型。

Conclusion: 基于语言的数字孪生框架为认知健康监测提供了一种可扩展、非侵入式且高度个性化的新范式，能够有效模拟老年人的认知表现。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [The Kernel's Write: Application Read-Only Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26138)
*Hui Sub Shim,Katherine Mohr,Philip Levis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 为弥补LtRAM硬件管理层带来的性能损耗，本文提出了AROM（应用只读内存）设计，将LtRAM的管理交给操作系统而非硬件，通过CoW机制处理写操作，从而实现高性能、高密度的大内存部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LtRAM（如Intel Optane）通过硬件翻译层屏蔽其写入延迟、耐用性和写入粒度等缺点，但这会引入额外的硬件延迟，限制了数据中心对高密度、低成本大内存的需求。

Method: 提出“应用只读内存（AROM）”设计范式：LtRAM页对应用层强制设为只读，应用层对LtRAM的写操作触发缺页异常（Page Fault），由操作系统负责将页迁回DRAM后再执行写操作，从而简化DIMM硬件控制器设计并消除其固有的额外延迟。

Result: 该方法简化了硬件复杂度，通过将写操作和管理逻辑转移至OS层，在保持读性能接近纯DRAM的情况下，实现了LtRAM在大规模数据中心环境下的有效利用。

Conclusion: 通过将LtRAM的管理责任从硬件控制器卸载到操作系统，并利用Copy-on-Write（CoW）机制维持“应用只读内存（AROM）”不变性，可以在不牺牲读性能的前提下，发挥LtRAM的密度和成本优势。

Abstract: Alongside power, DRAM has become a major limiting factor in datacenter growth. As DRAM's cost-per-bit has plateaued over the past decade, a class of emerging memory technologies, called Long-term RAM (LtRAM), offers a path to denser and cheaper main memory.
  However, LtRAM has three main drawbacks: asymmetric read/write latencies, limited endurance, and coarse write granularity. In an attempt to isolate software from these drawbacks, LtRAM technologies such as Intel Optane copy an approach from flash devices and introduce a translation layer that manages wear-leveling, address remapping, and read/write caching. Prior experimental studies have found these operations add significantly to LtRAM latency.
  Rather than making LtRAM look like DRAM, we propose redesigning the hardware/software interface to offload more responsibility to the operating system. This design hinges on one central property, Application Read-Only Memory (AROM): LtRAM pages are read-only to applications and written only by the OS during page migrations. AROM is enforced by leveraging copy-on-write (CoW): application writes to LtRAM trigger a fault that migrates the page back to DRAM before the store is applied. This invariant allows us to shift LtRAM management from the on-DIMM controller to the operating system, drastically simplifying the DIMM's hardware. With this approach, we aim to match the performance of pure DRAM on read-mostly workloads while delivering LtRAM's density and cost advantages.

</details>


### [149] [Mitigating High-Frequency Geometric Noise in Non-Parametric 1-Bit Sparse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26137)
*Lars Kopp*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 一种低功耗、无乘法器的神经形态计算框架，通过将数据映射至高维超完备空间并进行二进制编码，结合硬件低通滤波器有效解决了重构中的高频噪点问题，为边缘AI提供高效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决神经形态计算中能源效率问题，旨在规避高能耗的浮点运算。

Method: 提出了一种确定性、非参数化的双流形执行框架。通过将128维整数向量映射到1024维超完备空间，并使用8位有界变换矩阵和硬激活阈值生成1比特稀疏二进制群体编码。针对重构过程中出现的高频几何噪声，引入硬件级数字低通滤波器进行滤除。

Result: 证明了非参数映射中的高频几何噪声与核心信号拓扑严格正交，通过硬件滤除机制，重构误差可被降至近零范围。发现了低复杂度输入函数的重构误差反而高于高复杂度函数的“算法复杂度悖论”。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了一种极其稳定、无乘法器的替代方案，可用于边缘AI领域，并通过多尺度复杂度和分类阈值验证其有效性。

Abstract: Energy-efficient neuromorphic computing requires alternative data-encoding paradigms that bypass power-hungry floating-point operations. This paper evaluates a deterministic, non-parametric dual-manifold execution framework that maps dense 128-element integer vectors - representing digitized multi-frequency trigonometric waveforms - into a 1024-dimensional overcomplete space using 8-bit bounded transformation matrices. By enforcing a hard activation threshold, the system yields an ultra-sparse, 1-bit binary population code where y belongs to the set (0, 1)^1024. We identify and address a critical phenotypic artifact of this non-parametric mapping: the emergence of high-frequency geometric noise during linear reconstruction. Furthermore, we document an algorithmic complexity paradox where low-complexity input functions yield significantly higher reconstruction errors than highly complex, high-degree trigonometric combinations. Because the underlying basis functions operate as purely objective mathematical entities without statistical priors regarding signal smoothness, this geometric noise is proven to be strictly orthogonal to the core signal topology. Consequently, we demonstrate that a low-overhead, hardware-level digital low-pass filter completely eliminates this artifact, reducing reconstruction errors to near-zero bounds even under tight overcompleteness constraints. This architecture validates a highly stable, multiplier-free alternative to traditional deep learning hardware for edge-AI applications, verified through comprehensive empirical evaluations across varying complexity scales and classification thresholds (tau = 10 and tau = 100).

</details>


### [150] [Toward Mitigating Process-Induced Performance Degradation in 3.5D Heterogeneous Packages via Pre-Silicon Firmware Co-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26176)
*Chi Fei Chung,Nikolai Nedovodin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了针对Intel 3.5D封装的XRM-SSD V7.0固件调度层，通过预测性热提示技术优化电源管理，提升了算力效率并大幅缩减了保守的EDA设计限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模3.5D异构集成封装（如Intel Foveros Direct 3D等）中，面临高密度互连和热管理挑战，需解决由此带来的热流限制、CPO光谱漂移及泄漏电流等问题。

Method: 1. 开发了物理感知的预测性固件调度层（XRM-SSD V24/V7.0）；2. 采用详细的热-电协同仿真，涵盖90,000步LLM推理数据集；3. 引入主动式负载密度驱动的热提示（20-50ms前瞻）；4. 使用N x N热耦合矩阵和双极核（two-pole kernel）处理多芯片架构；5. 进行2,000次蒙特卡洛试验以验证工艺变异下的鲁棒性。

Result: 1. 热负荷相关性达到R^2=0.9911；2. CPO光谱漂移控制在0.36nm以内；3. HBM泄漏电流限制在1MB/hr内；4. 实现了20-30%的可用算力提升，并将EDA保护带（guard-band）减少了65-68%。

Conclusion: 固件-硬件协同优化（firmware-hardware co-optimization）是缓解先进3.5D封装物理限制的有效途径。XRM-SSD V7.0通过主动式热提示机制，显著提升了计算密度并降低了EDA保护带要求。

Abstract: This paper presents a pre-silicon analysis of XRM-SSD V24/V7.0, a physics-aware predictive firmware scheduling layer for Intel's 3.5D heterogeneous integrated packages (Foveros Direct 3D + PowerVia + EMIB-T + UCIe + HBM5). Using detailed thermal-electrical co-simulation over a 90,000-step LLM inference dataset, we show that proactive workload-density-driven thermal hinting (20-50 ms look-ahead) enables pre-positioning of PowerVia voltage rails. Key results include a thermal-load correlation of R^2 = 0.9911, compensated CPO spectral drift below 0.36 nm (21% of TSMC tolerance budget), and HBM leakage current clamped below 1 MB/hr across all load states. Monte Carlo analysis (N=2,000 trials) confirms robustness under process variation. V7.0 extends the framework to multi-tile architectures with an N x N thermal coupling matrix and two-pole kernel. The approach demonstrates potential for 20-30% released compute and 65-68% EDA guard-band reduction. All metrics are engineering projections from pre-silicon characterization. Silicon validation on Intel 18A platforms is pending. This work highlights firmware-hardware co-optimization as an effective approach to mitigating physical limits in advanced 3.5D packaging.

</details>


### [151] [CVA6-RT: an Open-Source Time-Predictable RV64 Processor for Mixed-Criticality Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26177)
*Enrico Zelioli,Christopher Reinwardt,Nils Wistoff,Robert Balas,Alessandro Ottaviano,Luca Benini,Angelo Garofalo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CVA6-RT，一种针对 CVA6 RISC-V 内核的实时微架构扩展，通过 TLB 锁定、可重配置 Scratchpad 和硬件加速中断机制，显著降低了中断延迟并提升了执行确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准 CVA6 内核在实时应用中面临的 WCET（最坏情况执行时间）无法界定以及执行时间抖动过大的问题，使其能够满足硬实时系统的严格 Timing 要求。

Method: 1. 实现 TLB 分区与锁定以确保地址转换的可预测性；2. 实现 L1 缓存的动态可重配置 Scratchpad 模式，保障确定性访存；3. 引入增强型中断控制器与硬件辅助上下文堆栈，优化中断处理流程。

Result: 实现了 12 个时钟周期的中断延迟，较基准 CVA6 降低了 10 倍，并达到了与 Arm Cortex-M 系列微控制器相当的实时响应水平。

Conclusion: CVA6-RT 通过微架构优化，成功在保持 RV64GCH 指令集兼容性的同时，实现了可预测的确定性实时性能，使其在保持高性能 RISC-V 内核特性的前提下，具备了媲美工业级实时微控制器的低中断延迟和执行确定性。

Abstract: This work presents CVA6-RT, a real-time micro-architectural extension of the CVA6 core to bound worst-case latency and reduce task's timing execution variability. CVA6-RT implements the rv64gch ISA and features advanced support for real-time execution, including TLB partitioning and locking for predictable address translation, a dynamically reconfigurable scratchpad mode in the L1 caches for deterministic memory access, and low-latency interrupt handling via an enhanced interrupt controller combined with hardware-assisted context stacking. With real-time features enabled, CVA6-RT achieves an interrupt latency of 12 cycles, comparable to that of simpler Arm Cortex-M microcontrollers, and 10x lower than the baseline CVA6 core.

</details>


### [152] [Nanoelectromechanical Systems (NEMS) for Hardware Security in Advanced Packaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26426)
*Himanandhan Reddy Kottur,Pavanbabu Arjunamahanthi,M. Shafkat M. Khan,Liton Kumar Biswas,Nitin Varshney,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用纳米机电系统（NEMS）构建新型硬件安全原语，通过利用其纳米尺度的物理随机性和机械特性，为半导体硬件提供抗篡改、防伪及高鲁棒性的安全防护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对半导体制造和先进封装过程中日益严峻的硬件安全威胁（如篡改、伪造和供应链渗透），迫切需要开发新型物理机制以增强硬件的安全性与可追溯性。

Method: 利用纳米机电系统(NEMS)的特性，包括但不限于NEMS-PUF（物理不可克隆函数）、形状记忆材料、谐振指纹识别及物理解锁架构，通过引入纳米尺度制造差异和机械随机性来实现硬件安全。

Result: 研究表明，基于NEMS的安全原语能够显著增强抵御逆向工程、侧信道攻击和环境退化的能力，且具备与标准半导体工艺集成的潜力。

Conclusion: NEMS技术凭借其独特的物理鲁棒性和低功耗特性，为半导体硬件安全提供了一种新型、可扩展且可靠的解决方案，能够有效应对供应链中的多种安全威胁。

Abstract: As hardware security threats escalate across semiconductor manufacturing and advanced packaging, there is a growing need for novel physical mechanisms to counter sophisticated attacks such as tampering, counterfeiting, and supply chain infiltration. This paper presents Nanoelectromechanical Systems (NEMS) as an emerging class of hardware security primitives that enable physical assurance, tamper detection, and authentication at the device level. Leveraging mechanisms such as NEMS-based Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs), shape memory materials, resonance-based fingerprints, and physical unlocking architectures, these systems offer enhanced resilience to reverse engineering, side-channel attacks, and environmental degradation. By harnessing mechanical unpredictability and fabrication-induced nanoscale variability, NEMS technologies introduce a physically robust and low-power alternative to conventional digital security methods. Their seamless integration into standard semiconductor workflows paves the way for scalable, verifiable, and secure solutions across defense, aerospace, critical infrastructure, and consumer electronics.

</details>


### [153] [GRAINS: Storage-Aware Algorithm-Architecture Co-Design Enabling High-Performance and Low-Cost Graph-Based Genome Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26468)
*Nika Mansouri Ghiasi,Harun Mustafa,Talu Güloglu,Rakesh Nadig,Konstantina Koliogeorgi,Susana Rebolledo Ruiz,Marc Rautmann,Furkan Eris,Mohammad Sadrosadati,Jisung Park,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GRAINS 是首个针对大规模基因组图分析的存储内处理系统，通过优化数据流和计算调度，极大缓解了I/O瓶颈，实现了显著的性能提升与能效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组图分析在大规模数据库处理中面临巨大的存储带宽瓶颈（因大量低重用数据搬移），且现有的SSD存储内处理方案无法适配基因组图特殊的访问模式和计算需求。

Method: 1. 存储感知算法-架构协同设计（Algorithm-Architecture Co-design）；2. 针对基因组图特点的批处理与执行流优化；3. 利用闪存内处理技术避免低重用数据的无效传输；4. 设计轻量级调度技术，通过重用现有SSD结构挖掘闪存芯片并行度。

Result: 相比现有最优软件基准，SPE速度提升2.7x-47.8x，能耗降低4.4x-31.6x；相比硬件加速基准，速度提升1.5x-17.0x，能耗降低3.1x-20.7x。

Conclusion: GRAINS 通过存储内计算（In-storage and In-flash processing）和软硬件协同设计，有效解决了大规模基因组图分析中的数据搬移瓶颈，显著提升了处理效率并降低了能耗。

Abstract: Graph-based representations of genome sequences have emerged as a powerful approach for representing massive genomic databases in an expressive and efficient way. Despite their benefits, analysis on large-scale genome graphs incurs significant data movement overhead from the storage system due to accessing large amounts of low-reuse data. Processing data directly inside the storage device can be a fundamental solution for mitigating this overhead. However, none of the existing tools for graph-based genome analysis can be efficiently used inside the storage system due to the limited internal hardware resources in modern SSDs. At the same time, prior storage-centric systems developed for (i) traditional, linear non-graph-based genome analysis or (ii) conventional, non-genomic graph analysis are not suitable for the unique data structures and access patterns of graph-based genome analysis.
  We propose GRAINS, the first system for analysis with large-scale genome graphs in storage. Through our detailed examination of typical analysis pipelines that operate on genome graphs, we perform storage-aware algorithm-architecture co-design to (i) make these pipelines more storage-friendly and (ii) further improve performance, energy-efficiency, and cost via in-storage and in-flash processing. GRAINS's co-design is based on three key aspects. First, we propose a new batching and execution flow, based on unique features of genome graphs. Second, via in-flash and in-storage processing, we avoid transferring low-reused flash pages. Third, to leverage the full parallelism of flash dies, we design an effective, yet lightweight, scheduling technique, enabled by re-purposing the existing SSD structures. GRAINS provides 2.7x-47.8x speedup (4.4x-31.6x energy reduction) over the state-of-the-art software baselines, and 1.5x-17.0x speedup (3.1x-20.7x energy reduction) over a hardware-accelerated baseline.

</details>


### [154] [SegFold: Accelerating Sparse GEMM with a Fine-Grained Dynamic Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26701)
*Xinrui Wu,Hanyu Wang,Jason Cong,Tony Nowatzki*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种引入动态调度与任务重映射机制的SpGEMM数据流及加速器架构（SegFold），有效克服了静态数据流在资源利用和负载平衡上的弊端，大幅提升了稀疏矩阵乘法的执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SpGEMM加速器（CPU/GPU及专用硬件）主要依赖于静态数据流（如内积、外积算法），导致在处理不同密度和规模的稀疏矩阵时，无法有效平衡数据重用与负载均衡，造成资源浪费和性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种名为“Segment”的动态数据流模型，并以此设计了加速器“SegFold”。SegFold通过包含精细化局部窗口重用识别功能的内存控制器，以及能够动态重映射任务的合并网络，实现了计算过程中的动态调度与任务平衡。

Result: 在多种矩阵密度和规模的测试下，SegFold相比最先进的SpGEMM加速器实现了几何平均1.95倍的加速，相比最优的静态数据流配置则实现了5.3倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过在SpGEMM数据流中引入动态性（动态调度与任务重映射），突破了静态数据流在重用和负载均衡方面的局限，显著提升了硬件加速器的能效比，证明了动态调度在稀疏计算加速中的重要价值。

Abstract: Generalized sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpGEMM) is critical in many domains. Existing CPUs and GPUs, as well as specialized accelerators, rely on static dataflows (e.g., inner product, outer product, Gustavson, etc.). Each static dataflow sacrifices some data reuse opportunities and imposes constraints on load balance.
  To address this inefficiency, we extend the typical SpGEMM dataflows by considering dynamism. Specifically, we add fine-grained dynamic scheduling to optimize reuse and reduce resource contention. We also develop dynamic remapping of partially completed work to improve load balance and parallelism. These ideas are formalized into a specific dataflow called Segment. To demonstrate Segment, we codesign a SpGEMM accelerator called SegFold. SegFold includes a memory controller that identifies fine-grained reuse opportunities in a local window of the stationary input array and exploits them through dynamic work assignment. It also incorporates a merge network that routes inputs to appropriate processing elements (PEs) for computation while dynamically remapping the work assigned to each PE to balance load.
  Across diverse densities and matrix sizes, SegFold achieves a geometric-mean $1.95\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art SpGEMM accelerators and $5.3\times$ over the best static dataflow configuration, demonstrating that adding dynamism to the dataflow design space unlocks reuse and load-balance gains that no static scheduling choice can achieve in isolation.

</details>


### [155] [Residual GPU Cache State on Apple M4 Pro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27098)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过实验量化了 Apple M4 Pro 上 GPU 对 CPU 共享缓存的置换效应，证实了 GPU 执行后的性能损耗主要源于缓存竞争，并提出了一种有效的恢复性补救策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon 的统一内存架构虽已公开，但对于 GPU 命令完成后留下的缓存状态缺乏文档记录，需要量化 GPU 任务对 CPU 共享缓存的具体影响。

Method: 利用经过验证的测量流水线（基于 STREAM/BabelStream），通过 8192 字节系统级缓存占用模式进行同步 Metal 实验；采用 PMU 性能计数器及 IOReport 直方图进行硬件分析，对比不同 GPU 内存占用下的 CPU 访问延迟。

Result: 实验发现 GPU 占用大内存足迹后会导致 CPU 首次访问延迟显著增加，且该现象在多次遍历后消失（证实为缓存置换效应）；同时发现高优先级 CPU 流量对 GPU 的干扰较小，表明背景 QoS 管理较为高效。

Conclusion: 在 Apple M4 Pro 上存在可重现的 GPU 后缓存置换窗口，该效应源于共享缓存区被 GPU 占用，而非同步 DRAM 争用；研究提供了一种简单的单次扫描软件恢复机制以缓解该性能损失。

Abstract: Apple silicon exposes unified CPU-GPU memory, but the cache state left after a completed GPU command is not documented. This paper characterizes that phase boundary on a 14-core Apple M4 Pro. We validate the measurement pipeline against unmodified STREAM 5.10 and BabelStream 5.0, then adapt an 8192-byte system-level-cache occupancy pattern to a synchronized Metal experiment. A GPU kernel touches 0 to 512 MiB and finishes before a 16 MiB CPU probe begins. The first CPU traversal is slower after large GPU footprints, while a second traversal removes most of the cost, showing residual shared-cache displacement rather than simultaneous DRAM contention. A separate matched-block experiment measures GPU slowdown under high-priority CPU traffic and finds background QoS close to baseline. Root PMU measurements and public IOReport histograms provide hardware grounding: they distinguish L1D refill sectors from software cache-line size, expose page-offset-dependent conflict behavior, and separate performance-core, efficiency-core, and AGX demand. The results identify a reproducible post-GPU cache-displacement window on M4 Pro and quantify a simple one-pass software recovery mechanism.

</details>


### [156] [Evaluating Architectural Trade-offs in CGRAs: The Impact of Scratchpad Memory and Heterogeneity on Compute-Intensive Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27240)
*María José Belda,Lara Orlandic,Fernando Castro,Miguel Peón-Quirós,Katzalin Olcoz,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过对比同构与异构CGRA架构，揭示了SPM对减少内存交通的关键作用，并指出同构设计在面积效率和广泛矩阵计算任务上具有优势，为边缘算力架构设计提供了权衡指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算（如Vision Transformers）对高吞吐量流处理的需求，与受限的功耗和面积资源之间的矛盾，驱动了对CGRA架构设计（特别是处理单元异构性和数据复用）进行优化分析的需求。

Method: 对比了两种CGRA评估配置：基准同构架构与集成专用功能单元及暂存内存（SPM）的异构架构；在FFT、GEMM及癫痫检测Transformer工作负载上进行了实验评估。

Result: SPM将内存流量减少了8倍；同构架构在面积上比异构架构节省了4.4x至8.2x，并在矩阵计算中实现了最高5倍的性能提升；异构架构在数据重排任务中具备更好的能效。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个架构路线图，旨在根据边缘计算工作负载的算术强度、性能目标和资源限制来指导CGRA架构的设计选择（异构性与本地数据复用）。

Abstract: Modern edge computing applications, particularly high-throughput stream processing like Vision Transformers (ViTs), demand massive spatial parallelism and efficient data movement under tight power and area constraints. Coarse-Grained Reconfigurable Architectures (CGRAs) offer a promising paradigm to balance performance, flexibility, and energy efficiency. This paper analyzes the impact of two critical CGRA design choices: processing element heterogeneity and local data reuse support. We evaluate essential computational kernels (Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and General Matrix Multiply (GEMM)) alongside an end-to-end seizure detection transformer workload across two distinct configurations: a baseline homogeneous architecture and a heterogeneous evolution integrating specialized functional units with an Scratchpad Memory (SPM). Our evaluation demonstrates that the SPM significantly optimizes data movement, reducing memory traffic eightfold compared to a memory-less design. While the heterogeneous architecture achieves superior energy efficiency for data-shuffling tasks, the homogeneous design minimizes area overhead by 4.4x to 8.2x relative to state-of-the-art CGRAs. Furthermore, it sustains a 700 MHz operating frequency, enabling up to a 5x execution speedup over the heterogeneous configuration during matrix computations. Ultimately, this work provides an architectural roadmap for selecting CGRA fabrics based on the arithmetic intensity, performance goals, and resource envelopes of edge-scale workloads.

</details>


### [157] [CHIA: An open-source framework for principled, agentic AI-driven hardware/software co-design research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27350)
*Angela Cui,Ferran Hermida-Rivera,Jack Toubes,Raghav Gupta,Jim Fang,Chengyi Lux Zhang,Ella Schwarz,Junha Kim,Yakun Sophia Shao,Borivoje Nikolic,Christopher W. Fletcher,Sagar Karandikar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CHIA 是一个专为软硬件协同设计打造的开源代理 AI 框架，通过引入可扩展的“CHIA 循环”工作流，解决了该领域 AI 应用部署难的问题，并支持在大规模异构系统上进行敏捷、严谨的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 AI 在硬件/软件协同设计领域潜力巨大，但目前的应用大多局限于小规模、孤立的场景，缺乏一套能够支持复杂、敏捷且可扩展研发工作流的通用基础设施。

Method: 提出 CHIA 框架，通过“CHIA 循环”（有向环图）将设计工具、模拟器、AI 模型及进化算法等节点连接；提供 CHIA 库以集成主流工具（如 Chipyard, gem5, FireSim 等），并实现跨异构计算平台的大规模容错部署与性能分析。

Result: 通过五个实践案例（包括 RTL-gem5 对齐、LLM 生成微架构功能、IPC 感知路径优化等）展示了该框架在硬件设计全流程中的实际应用潜力与灵活性。

Conclusion: CHIA 作为一个开源框架，成功解决了在复杂硬件/软件协同设计中部署智能体 AI 的工程瓶颈，通过模块化和可扩展的“CHIA 循环”设计，为在该领域进行系统性、科学性的 AI 研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence shows great promise for radically improving the pace of innovation in hardware/software co-design research across computer architecture, systems, compilers, and VLSI. Thus far, however, applications of AI in these contexts have generally been demonstrated in isolated settings on small-scale problems, due to the difficulty of designing and deploying complex AI-infused hardware and software development workflows.
  This paper introduces CHIA, an open-source hardware/software co-design framework for agile and principled research on the application of AI to co-design. CHIA treats the productive construction and scalable deployment of the co-design flow itself as a first-class objective. In CHIA, agentic AI-driven hardware and software design flows are expressed as \textit{CHIA loops}: directed cyclic graphs whose nodes execute various system-on-chip design tools, microarchitectural simulators, software build systems, AI models, evolutionary coding agents, and more. The \textit{CHIA library} provides node implementations for many popular tools, including Chipyard, gem5, ChampSim, FireSim, Hammer (thus several commercial ASIC CAD tools), Vivado, AlphaEvolve, AdaEvolve, and many others.
  CHIA also provides a broad set of features to conduct principled science around these flows. These include isolation between AI models and hardware tools, profiling mechanisms, fault-tolerant execution, and reliability at scale across hundreds of heterogeneous systems (CPUs, FPGAs, GPUs, etc., across public cloud/on-prem.).
  To showcase CHIA, we present five CHIA loops as case studies: (1) automatic RTL-to-gem5 simulator alignment, (2) LLM-driven implementation of microarchitectural features in RTL, (3) agentic, IPC-aware critical path optimization, (4) evolutionary architectural discovery, and (5) maintainer-friendly agentic GitHub issue fixing.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Query Cost Model Calibration in Confidential Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26385)
*Qihan Zhang,Mengyuan Li,Ibrahim Sabek*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该研究针对机密虚拟机（CVM）中数据库查询性能低下的问题，识别出查询优化器缺乏感知 CVM 特性的硬件假设是根本原因，并提出了一种通过模型化数据移动和 RMP 开销的轻量级代价校准方法，显著提升了 CVM 环境下的数据库查询效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机密计算的普及，将数据库部署在 CVM（如 AMD SEV-SNP）中已成为主流，但由此带来的硬件-软件不匹配导致查询优化器采用了过时的成本假设，造成了严重的性能开销。现有的研究多停留在对性能损耗的基准测试层面，缺乏有效的优化手段。

Method: 提出一种轻量级 CVM 感知（CVM-aware）代价校准方法。该方法通过建模数据移动和 RMP (Reverse Map Table) 相关转换这两大主要开销，利用查询优化器中已有的物理代理变量进行成本调整，以更新优化器的决策依据。

Result: 实验表明，该校准机制能有效缩小 KVM 与 CVM 之间的性能差距，最高可恢复 48% 的性能损失，在部分工作负载下甚至能够超越传统的 KVM 基准表现。

Conclusion: 在机密虚拟机（CVM）中，传统的查询优化器因基于不再适用的非加密环境假设而导致性能下降。通过引入轻量级的、感知CVM特性的代价校准机制，能够显著弥合KVM与CVM之间的性能差距，验证了针对机密计算进行查询优化器层面适配的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: With the growing adoption of Confidential Computing, running databases in confidential virtual machines (CVMs) such as AMD SEV-SNP has become an attractive way to protect sensitive cloud data with minimal changes to legacy DBMSs. However, analytical queries in such CVMs often suffer substantial overhead, and prior database work has largely stopped at benchmarking these slowdowns rather than optimizing them. We show that this problem stems from a hardware-software mismatch: query optimizers still rely on KVM-oriented (non-encrypted VM) cost assumptions that no longer hold in CVMs. To address this, we propose a lightweight CVM-aware cost calibration. It models two dominant sources of optimizer-facing overhead: data movement and RMP-related translation using simple physical proxies already available to the optimizer. Experiments show that the calibration significantly narrows the KVM/CVM performance gap, recovering up to 48 percent performance and even outperforming the KVM baseline on some workloads.

</details>


### [159] [3D Spatial Pattern Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26465)
*Nicole R. Schneider,Avik Das,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Abhijeet Ghodgaonkar,Hanan Samet,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展空间模式匹配至三维空间的方法，通过改进子图匹配算法处理三维距离关系，并发布了相关数据集以推动针对三维实体空间搜索的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间模式匹配研究局限于二维平面，无法处理具有高度信息的现实世界实体（如建筑物等），存在应用局限性。

Method: 1. 将二维空间模式匹配扩展至三维空间；2. 提出了基于距离关系的3D空间模式匹配的子图匹配算法；3. 构建并发布了两个3D空间模式匹配数据集（一个合成数据集，一个来自德国汉堡的真实三维建筑数据）。

Result: 在合成数据集及德国汉堡的真实三维建筑数据集上进行了测试，验证了所提算法的有效性，并为该领域后续研究提供了基准性能指标。

Conclusion: 本文成功将空间模式匹配从二维扩展至三维，定义了三维空间模式匹配问题，并提出了相应的子图匹配算法，为处理具有高度信息的现实世界实体匹配提供了基础框架及基准数据集。

Abstract: Spatial pattern matching is the process of matching query entities and constraints with database entities and relations. It has many applications, including similar region search, housing market search, landmark search, and road network matching. To our knowledge, all existing spatial pattern matching approaches frame the problem in a 2 dimensional space, where entities lie in a cartesian plane and relationships defined between them are contained in 2 dimensions. However, this problem framing has significant limitations when searching for real world entities that have height in addition to position. To address this limitation, we extend spatial pattern matching to 3 dimensions and provide a generalized definition of the problem. We describe a subgraph matching algorithm capable of resolving 3D spatial patterns over distance relations and release two 3D spatial pattern matching datasets, one synthetic and one containing real 3D building data from the city of Hamburg, Germany. We test our subgraph matching algorithm on both datasets and present results as a baseline for future methods to build upon.

</details>


### [160] [EcoTable: Cost-effective Table Integration in Data Lakes for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26613)
*Yuhui Wang,Jinqi Liu,Chengliang Chai,Hangyu Zhao,Yuhao Deng,Yuyu Luo,Xin Tang,Ye Yuan,Guoren Wang,Fengjin Wang,Lei Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EcoTable 提出了一种基于自然语言查询的数据集成框架，通过构建图结构与 LLM 协同优化，实现了从数据湖到满足特定分析需求表的自动化集成，显著提升了查询准确度并大幅降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ETL 流程过度依赖人工预定义模式，难以灵活应对数据湖中多样化的原始格式（CSV/Parquet）以及多变的、不可预知的分析需求；且现有方法在处理复杂查询时存在效率瓶颈与高昂的 LLM 使用成本。

Method: 1. 构建包含表（节点）与连接概率（边）的全局图结构；2. 表识别层：基于两阶段模式链接（Schema Linking）识别相关表；3. 基于图的验证层：将问题建模为 Steiner 树搜索，自动发现连接路径与转换逻辑；4. 表转换层：利用 LLM 生成执行数据集成与转换的代码，并采取策略优化 LLM 调用频率与开销。

Result: 在 4 个真实场景数据集及 200 多条查询的基准测试中，EcoTable 相比现有最优基准方法（SOTA），准确率提升超过 30%，且 LLM 调用成本降低了 5 倍。

Conclusion: EcoTable 证明了利用大语言模型（LLM）进行数据集成是高效且可行的，通过图结构优化搜索空间可显著降低计算成本并提升查询准确率，为解决复杂多样的数据湖集成问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: The diverse formats of CSV and Parquet files in data lakes pose a significant challenge to traditional ETL, which relies on data engineers to pre-define a target database schema and build a complex pipeline for data integration. Moreover, with this approach, the integrated data often cannot support various analytical needs, as the predefined schema does not necessarily satisfy the table format or join relationships required to answer unforeseen queries. To address this, we propose EcoTable, the first natural language-based data integration framework. Given a set of user-specified natural language queries, EcoTable automatically integrates the tables into a form that adequately supports the corresponding SQL queries. EcoTable achieves this by leveraging the semantic understanding and complex reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Moreover, EcoTable addresses the scalability and cost issues introduced by expensive LLM inferences with a set of novel ideas. First, EcoTable introduces a graph to represent the overall search space, where nodes represent tables and edges carry weights indicating join likelihood produced by a lightweight deep learning model. On top of this graph data structure, EcoTable designs three components to achieve our goal: (1) the table identification layer aims to identify relevant tables via a two-stage schema linking based on user queries; (2) the graph-based validation layer aims to discover significant join paths, including necessary data transformations and bridging tables, by modeling the problem as Steiner tree searches; and (3) the table transformation layer generates transformation code to implement the joins using LLMs. We construct 4 real-world benchmark datasets with more than 200 queries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EcoTable outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines, increasing accuracy by more than 30% and cutting LLM invocation costs by 5 times.

</details>


### [161] [BtrLog: Low-Latency Logging for Cloud Database Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27051)
*Maximilian Kuschewski,Lam-Duy Nguyen,Matthias Jasny,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis,Muhammad El-Hindi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BtrLog 是一种高性能、低成本且通用的云数据库日志服务。它通过 SSD 集群处理低延迟日志提交，并利用异步归档将数据写入对象存储，成功解决了传统云存储方案在性能与成本之间的折中问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的云数据库 WAL 持久化方案存在局限：远程块存储（如 EBS）延迟高且成本昂贵；对象存储（Object Storage）延迟极高且不适合高频小写；专有的日志后端往往与特定数据库引擎（DBMS）耦合，缺乏通用性，无法有效复用。

Method: 1. 采用基于 SSD 的日志节点集群，通过多数派共识（Quorum-based）实现快速的日志持久化；2. 使用单次网络往返（single network round trip）来降低提交延迟并减轻“拖尾效应”；3. 采用异步分段归档机制，将日志数据从 SSD 定期卸载至低成本的对象存储中，从而规避延迟敏感路径。

Result: 实验表明，BtrLog 在延迟方面表现优于 EBS，并且在集成到数据库管理系统（DBMS）后，显著提升了端到端的事务处理吞吐量。

Conclusion: BtrLog 是一个可复用的云原生日志服务，通过结合 SSD 节点的高性能日志追加和对象存储的低成本归档，有效解决了目前云数据库日志存储在性能、成本和通用性之间的权衡难题。

Abstract: Cloud database systems cannot rely on instance-local disks for write-ahead logging (WAL) durability, forcing WAL onto remote storage. Existing options are unsatisfying: remote block storage like EBS is easy to adopt but adds substantial write latency and cost, while object storage offers excellent durability and low storage cost but is impractical for OLTP due to high latency and per-append cost. Many cloud-native databases, therefore, depend on purpose-built logging backends, which are typically proprietary and tightly coupled to engine-specific replication and recovery protocols, limiting reuse. We present BtrLog, a reusable cloud logging service that combines low-latency durable appends with low-cost archival for the common single-writer architecture. BtrLog replicates log records across a quorum of SSD-backed log nodes in a single network round trip, reducing sensitivity to stragglers in commit latency. To minimize storage cost, log nodes archive records to object storage as large segments, which are written asynchronously and off the latency-critical write path. In our evaluation, BtrLog achieves lower latency than EBS and enables higher end-to-end transaction throughput when integrated into a DBMS.

</details>


### [162] [Understanding Domain-Aware Distribution Alignment in Budgeted Entity Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27342)
*Nicholas Pulsone,Gregory Goren,Roee Shraga*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文对低资源领域感知实体匹配方法BEACON进行了系统的实证研究，探讨了不同算法设计和数据量对模型效能的具体影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对低资源、领域感知实体匹配的研究虽表现出色，但缺乏对不同数据约束和监督水平下算法鲁棒性与行为机制的深入分析。

Method: 对BEACON（一种用于低资源、领域感知的实体匹配方法）进行实证评估，通过控制变量法系统研究不同算法选择与数据约束对模型表现的影响。

Result: 通过系列针对性实验，深入剖析了分布对齐技术的作用机制，并揭示了BEACON框架在不同数据配置下的性能表现规律。

Conclusion: BEACON框架的性能受到分布对齐策略和数据可用性程度的显著影响，明确了算法组件在不同约束条件下的实际贡献。

Abstract: Entity Matching (EM) is a core operation in the data integration pipeline, where records from different sources are compared to determine whether they refer to the same real-world entity. Recent work has incorporated domain information and low-resource learning techniques to better adapt EM systems to realistic settings. While these approaches have demonstrated strong performance, it remains unclear how they behave under varying data constraints and levels of supervision in practice. In this paper, we investigate a state-of-the-art method for low-resource, domain-aware EM--BEACON--and study how its performance is affected by different algorithmic choices and data availability conditions. We conduct a series of targeted experiments to evaluate these variations, providing deeper insight into the role of distribution alignment and the behavior of the BEACON framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Unsupervised Memory-Enhanced Video Transformers: Obstacle Detection for Autonomous Agricultural Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26151)
*Théo Biardeau,Anne-Sophie Capelle-Laizé,Salwan Alwan,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对农业机器人在动态场景中无法有效检测未知障碍物的安全难题，本文提出了一种基于 Transformer 和记忆机制的无监督异常检测方法 VMTAD，在保证高检测精度的同时也满足了实时作业需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业机器人感知技术存在局限：传统 LiDAR 难以检测作物冠层下的障碍物；基于监督学习的摄像头方法对未见过的障碍物泛化能力差；传统非监督异常检测难以应对移动平台带来的背景动态变化。因此，需要一种能够实时、无监督地识别动态农业场景中未知障碍物的方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为“视频记忆变换器异常检测”（VMTAD）的无监督学习方法。该方法采用 Transformer 架构并集成了一个专门的记忆模块，能够通过处理前序帧的编码表示来捕获时间上下文，从而有效克服机器人自身运动导致的动态环境挑战。模型仅在“正常”操作图像上进行训练，无需任何标签。

Result: 在 Grillion 农业机器人数据集上的测试表明，VMTAD 达到了 SOTA 性能（检测 AUROC 为 0.973，分割 AUROC 为 0.997）。其轻量化版本可实现 14ms 的实时推理速度，能够满足机器人的安全制动要求。

Conclusion: VMTAD 证明了利用 Transformer 和记忆模块处理动态农业场景的潜力，实现了高精度、实时的无监督异常检测，对于保障农业机器人在复杂环境下的作业安全具有重要意义。

Abstract: While autonomous rovers have become indispensable to precision farming, achieving consistent operational safety remains a critical challenge. Conventional safety sensors, such as LiDAR, fail to detect obstacles positioned below the plant canopy, posing a significant risk. While camera-based supervised learning methods can detect common objects, they perform poorly when faced with obstacles that were not present in their training data. Actual unsupervised anomaly detection offers a solution by learning the normal visual patterns of an environment, but often fails for the dynamic scenes captured by a moving rover.\\ This paper introduces Video Memory Transformers for Anomaly Detection (VMTAD), a fully unsupervised method designed for real-time obstacle detection in dynamic agricultural scenes. VMTAD utilizes a transformer-driven architecture augmented with a dedicated memory module. This memory module leverages temporal context by processing encoded representations of preceding frames. This approach enables the system to effectively address the dynamic context caused by the robot's movement. The model is trained using only images that represent normal operation, requiring no data labels.\\ VMTAD was rigorously evaluated on the 'Grillion' agricultural rover. On a challenging rapeseed dataset, VMTAD achieved state-of-the-art performance, reaching a 0.973 detection and 0.997 segmentation Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve. A lightweight variant provides an optimal balance of high accuracy and real-time inference (14 ms), which is critical for safety, as confirmed by our analysis of the rover's total stopping distance.

</details>


### [164] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Autonomous Microrobot Navigation and Intervention in Simulated Blood Capillaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26154)
*Jannik Drotleff,Samuel Tovey,Paul Hohenberger,Christoph Lohrmann,Julian Hoßbach,Konstantin Nikolaou,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究构建了高保真生物血管模拟环境，通过深度强化学习成功训练出微型机器人，使其能够克服血流动力学挑战，在复杂仿生环境中自主实现导航、靶向阻塞及血流修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微型机器人导航研究多基于简化模型，忽略了真实生物体内复杂的血流、血管分支和细胞障碍，限制了其在精准药物递送和血栓溶解等实际临床场景中的应用。

Method: 建立了一个物理真实度高的毛细血管网络模拟环境，涵盖了真实的流体动力学、红细胞动态交互及解剖学结构，并采用深度强化学习（Deep RL）算法进行导航策略训练。

Result: 研究揭示了微型机器人导航的物理边界（运动学限制）；代理（Agents）自主演化出多种通用导航策略（如运动-旋转、能量高效的搜索策略）；模型在无需重训练的情况下，成功实现了毛细血管的自主阻塞与疏通。

Conclusion: 研究证实，强化学习是开发复杂生物环境下自主微型机器人干预策略的可行框架。通过学习到的策略，微型机器人能够有效辅助修复血管血流平衡，为未来医疗微纳机器人的实际应用提供了理论依据和模拟基础。

Abstract: Autonomous microrobots navigating biological vasculature could enable targeted drug delivery and thrombolysis, yet training control policies for realistic environments remains an open challenge. Prior reinforcement learning (RL) studies of microrobotic navigation have been limited to idealized geometries that omit complex hydrodynamic flow fields, confined branching structures, and dense cellular obstacles found in vivo. Here, we develop a physically grounded simulation of a blood capillary network, incorporating realistic hydrodynamic flow fields, explicit red blood cell dynamics, and anatomically derived branching geometry, and train deep RL agents to navigate it via chemotaxis. We systematically map the physical limits of navigation across robot size and swimming speed, revealing a forbidden regime where Brownian motion and flow overcome propulsion. Successful agents independently discover multiple universal strategy types, including run-and-rotate and energy-efficient search-and-sit policies, regardless of robot parameters. Without retraining, these agents perform targeted blocking and unblocking of capillary flow, restoring throughput to healthy baseline levels. These results establish RL as a viable framework for developing autonomous microrobotic intervention strategies in complex biological environments.

</details>


### [165] [RMTL: Reinforced Micro-task Learning for Long-Horizon Manipulation with VLM Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26175)
*Anıl Can Ateş,Orhan Kahraman,Cihan Topal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了强化微任务学习（RMTL），通过将复杂操作分解为多个微任务并结合分层强化学习与多视角VLM奖励，克服了单一语言提示奖励函数在长程任务中信息稀疏的弊端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于VLM零样本奖励的方法在长程、多初始状态的操作任务中，单一全局文本提示往往导致奖励函数过于平坦（稀疏），难以引导机器人早期学习，且调优困难。

Method: 1. 将长程任务分解为多个微任务，每项任务对应具体的语言提示；2. 使用多视角VLM平均奖励以减少遮挡影响；3. 采用逆向课程学习（Reverse Curriculum）进行初始条件训练；4. 引入分层策略（Hierarchical Policy），从初期的规则驱动任务切换演进为完全习得的层级管理。

Result: 在Fetch机器人操作环境中，相比单一提示的VLM奖励，RMTL提供了信息量更丰富的奖励信号，显著提高了训练速度和任务执行成功率。

Conclusion: 通过将VLM奖励分解为更细粒度的微任务描述，RMTL显著提升了语言引导强化学习在复杂机器人操作任务中的扩展性和学习效率，证明了分层奖励设计的优越性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation often requires manually designing a dense reward function, which is difficult to tune and often fragile, or learning a reward from human demonstrations or preferences, which can be expensive. A recent line of work uses pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as zero-shot reward models, replacing these costs with a single text prompt. However, we argue that a single global prompt is too coarse for long-horizon manipulation tasks with randomized initial conditions. The single-prompt VLM reward is near-flat for much of the trajectory, making early progress hard for the agent to detect. We propose Reinforced Micro-Task Learning (RMTL), an approach that decomposes a manipulation task into a small set of language-described micro-tasks and trains the agent to switch between them. At each step, the agent receives a multi-view VLM reward computed using the prompt of the currently active micro-task and averaged across multiple camera views to reduce the effect of view-specific occlusions. A reverse curriculum gradually exposes the agent to harder initial conditions, while a PPO worker is first trained with a fixed distance-based rule that selects the active micro-task. We then replace this rule with a learned hierarchical manager, turning rule-based phase selection into a fully learned hierarchical policy. We instantiate RMTL on the Fetch manipulation environment using three short stage-specific prompts and without additional prompt tuning. Experiments show that RMTL provides more informative reward signals than single-prompt VLM rewards, enabling faster learning. These results suggest that decomposing VLM rewards into micro-task-specific language prompts can substantially improve the scalability of language-guided reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [166] [LiMoDE: Rethinking Lifelong Robot Manipulation from a Mixture-of-Dynamic-Experts Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26183)
*Zhihao Gu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiMoDE通过一种创新的动态混合专家（MoE）架构和两阶段学习策略，解决了机器人终身学习中技能复用困难及灾难性遗忘的问题，显著提升了机器人跨任务适应的效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法虽能缓解灾难性遗忘，但难以有效提取可复用技能并建模技能间的交互。旨在构建一个具备通用性、能利用先验知识进行持续任务自适应的机器人系统。

Method: 提出一种名为LiMoDE的两阶段学习框架：1. 多任务预训练阶段：利用基于运动信息激活不同异构专家的动态MoE结构学习先验知识；2. 任务自适应阶段：设计终身MoE适应机制，通过动态组合终身学习专家与冻结的预训练专家实现知识迁移。

Result: 在模拟终身学习基准测试和真实世界任务中表现优异，在仅增加少量可训练参数和推理开销的情况下，实现了卓越的任务性能和持续自适应能力。

Conclusion: LiMoDE通过引入动态混合专家（MoE）架构和两阶段学习方案，有效解决了机器人终身学习中灾难性遗忘与技能复用之间的矛盾，实现了高效的任务自适应。

Abstract: Building a generalist robot that can leverage prior knowledge for continuous task adaptation remains a significant challenge. Previous works alleviate the catastrophic forgetting problem by parameter-efficient fine-tuning for single-task adaptation. However, they fail to extract reusable skills and model the interaction with other skills effectively. Recent works try to address these issues by learning prompts. Differently, this paper presents an architectural perspective on the Lifelong Mixture of Dynamic Experts (\textit{LiMoDE}), a novel two-stage learning scheme for lifelong robot manipulation. Specifically, a dynamic MoE structure is first proposed in the multi-task pre-training stage to learn prior knowledge, where a varied number of heterogeneous experts are activated based on the motion information to address different short-term manipulations. Subsequently, in the task adaptation stage, we design a lifelong MoE adaptation mechanism % (LiMoEAM) that learns lifelong experts and dynamically combines them with frozen ones for new tasks, facilitating the knowledge transfer during adaptation. The proposed \textit{LiMoDE} is evaluated on both the simulated lifelong learning benchmark and real-world tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness in achieving superior performance and strong lifelong adaptation by introducing a moderate number of additional trainable parameters and inference overhead.

</details>


### [167] [Morphology-Specific Closed-Loop Control of Logarithmic-Spiral Continuum Arms via Online Jacobian Error Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26188)
*Partha Datta,Yi Jin,Wei Lin,C. Chase Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了首个针对对数螺旋连续体机械臂的任务空间闭环控制框架，通过引入在线雅可比误差补偿，解决了此类机器人因非线性形变和接触带来的模型不确定性问题，实现了高精度的轨迹跟踪与任务操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对数螺旋机器人虽具备良好的抓取能力，但缺乏逆运动学和闭环控制手段，限制了其在精准任务中的应用。

Method: 1. 构建了基于MuJoCo的带中心脊柱及等边肌腱驱动的分段模型；2. 推导了直接基于对数螺旋运动学的解析雅可比矩阵；3. 设计了结合Broyden割线更新与卡尔曼滤波的雅可比矩阵在线误差补偿机制。

Result: 在轨迹跟踪、姿态调节和扰动抑制等实验中表现优于分段恒定曲率（PCC）基准模型；成功验证了避障、自适应抓取及多臂协作任务。

Conclusion: 引入在线雅可比修正的闭环控制框架，极大提升了对数螺旋连续体机械臂在复杂环境下的任务执行精度与鲁棒性，证实了该形态与先进控制算法结合的有效性。

Abstract: Logarithmic spirals are ubiquitous in biological appendages and provide an attractive morphology for continuum manipulators capable of reaching, wrapping, and grasping. Recently reported logarithmic-spiral robots demonstrated scalable fabrication and versatile grasping but lacked inverse kinematics and closed-loop control. This work presents the first morphology-specific closed-loop task-space control framework for logarithmic-spiral continuum arms. A segmented tendon-driven model with a centerline backbone and equilateral tendon routing is developed in MuJoCo to capture tapered compliance and contact dynamics. An analytical task-space Jacobian is derived directly from the logarithmic-spiral kinematics and combined with online Jacobian error compensation using a Broyden secant update and Kalman-filter estimation. The resulting controller continuously corrects modeling errors arising from nonlinear deformation, contact, and geometric mismatch. The framework is validated through planar and spatial simulations, including trajectory tracking, attitude regulation, disturbance rejection, three-dimensional position tracking, and simultaneous position-orientation control. Compared with a piecewise-constant-curvature (PCC) baseline, the proposed method consistently reduces tracking errors, suppresses attitude drift, and maintains a bounded Jacobian estimation error. The controller is further applied to morphology-enabled manipulation tasks, including obstacle-assisted reach-wrap-release motions, adaptive whole-arm grasping, and cooperative multi-arm object handling. Results demonstrate that combining logarithmic-spiral morphology with online Jacobian compensation enables accurate, robust, and scalable control of highly underactuated continuum manipulators. The proposed framework establishes a physics-grounded baseline for future hardware implementation and learning-augmented soft robotic control.

</details>


### [168] [OmniContact: Chaining Meta-Skills via Contact Flow for Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26201)
*Runyi Yu,Xiaoyi Lin,Ji Ma,Yinhuai Wang,Koukou Luo,Jiahao Ji,Huayi Wang,Wenjia Wang,Runhan Zhang,Ping Tan,Ting Wu,Ruoli Dai,Qifeng Chen,Lei Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于“接触流（Contact Flow）”的分层框架，通过统一的接口实现人形机器人的长时程移动与操作，大幅提升了任务成功率、技能组合能力及故障恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人形机器人长时程移动操作中存在的问题：显式交互表示难以进行高层规划，而隐式技能嵌入缺乏可解释性，导致难以实现可靠的复杂技能链式组合与自主故障处理。

Method: 提出了一种分层框架，核心在于“接触流（Contact Flow, CF）”表征（关键人体轨迹+时间序列二值接触信号）。包括：1. CF-Track：低层策略，用于学习统一的移动操作技能库；2. CF-Gen：高层模块，用于启发式合成未来的接触流序列；3. 构建了OmniContact数据集（基于动作捕捉的HOI语料库）。

Result: 在Carry Box任务中成功率达到98.7%，在Push-Stack Boxes任务中达到76.5%，分别较基线提升了40.9%和66.5%；支持结合VLM实现复杂的语义任务分解。

Conclusion: OmniContact通过引入接触流（Contact Flow）表征，成功解决了人形机器人长时程移动操作中的技能组合与故障恢复难题，显著提升了任务成功率，并具备良好的语义任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation poses a dual challenge: it requires not only the robust execution of meta-skills but also their seamless, closed-loop chaining equipped with autonomous recovery. Existing approaches remain limited: explicit humanoid-object interaction representations offer precision but are notoriously difficult for high-level planning, whereas implicit skill embeddings are compact but lack the interpretability required for reliable composition. We propose \ours, a hierarchical framework centered on \textbf{contact flow (CF)}, a compact representation consisting of key body trajectories and time-series binary contact signals. Leveraging this shared interface, our low-level policy \textbf{CF-Track} learns a unified library of loco-manipulation skills, while our high-level module \textbf{CF-Gen} heuristically synthesizes future contact-flow sequences. To support this setting, we additionally collect the OmniContact dataset, a MoCap-based HOI corpus for humanoid loco-manipulation (Appendix~\ref{sec:dataset}). Together, they enable robust execution, autonomous failure recovery, and flexible composition of meta-skills for long-horizon tasks. Experiments show that OmniContact achieves \(98.7\%\) success on \textit{Carry Box} and \(76.5\%\) on \textit{Push-Stack Boxes}, outperforming prior baselines by average margins of \(40.9\%\) in meta-skill and \(66.5\%\) in skill chaining. Besides, our framework naturally integrates with VLMs for semantic task decomposition, enabling complex, semantically grounded loco-manipulation behaviors, such as arranging scattered boxes into a heart shape.

</details>


### [169] [RoboTales: ROBOTic Anthropomorphic LEarning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26213)
*Andrew Chen,Ju-Hung Chen,Phurinat Pinyomit,Alexis E. Block*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTales 是一套低成本的机器人木偶交互系统，通过将讲故事动画化，证明了具身木偶表现比单纯的手势交互更能提升参与度和学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统机器人讲故事时表现力不足的问题，通过引入具身木偶表演，为儿童教育提供一种比被动屏幕媒体更具吸引力的交互式学习体验。

Method: 开发了一个基于机器人（以 Baxter 为例）的低成本木偶表演系统，集成了语音同步、肢体手势和口型控制，并与纯手势模式进行了对比的试点研究。

Result: 实验结果显示，木偶授课模式在 HRIES（人机情感互动）评分上更高，且显著改善了受众对故事内容的记忆力。

Conclusion: RoboTales 提供了一种低成本、模块化的机器人讲故事方案，通过木偶表演增强了人机交互的参与度和故事理解力，在儿童教育领域具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: RoboTales is a low-cost robotic storytelling system that animates narratives using expressive sock puppetry. Implemented autonomously on a Baxter robot as a test case, RoboTales synchronizes narration, gestures, and mouth movements to perform character-driven stories. In a pilot study, puppet-based storytelling outperformed a gesture-only mode, producing higher HRIES ratings and improved story recall, suggesting that embodied puppetry enhances engagement and narrative comprehension. Designed to be modular and platform-agnostic, RoboTales can be adapted to other manipulators and offers a screen-free alternative to passive media, supporting future deployment in child-centered learning environments.

</details>


### [170] [TaskNPoint: How to Teach Your Humanoid to Hit a Backhand in Minutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26215)
*Blake Werner,Ilona Demler,Pietro Perona,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于“教练-学员”范式的机器人训练协议（TaskNPoint），通过聚焦动态任务中决定性的“交互窗口”，实现了仅凭单条人类演示即可快速训练机器人执行复杂动态技能，并具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前机器人学习动态技能（如网球回球）的瓶颈，认为盲目的大规模数据学习效率低下，应仿效人类通过教练辅导和针对性练习，重点掌握决定任务成败的“关键轨迹窗口”。

Method: 提出了TaskNPoint训练协议，模仿“教练-学员”关系；利用人类提供的离散技能集、单次演示、关键交互窗口标注及目标定义；在仿真环境中通过随机目标采样进行训练，从而实现对未见目标的零样本泛化。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上成功验证了打网球（正反手）、踢足球及物品放置等任务；仅需少量视频演示，在单GPU上训练不到一小时，且无需针对特定任务调整奖励函数（Reward Engineering）。

Conclusion: 通过将动态技能简化为对关键“交互窗口”的精准控制，并结合人类教练的少样本演示与仿真训练，能够实现人形机器人在现实世界中高效、零样本泛化的复杂动态任务学习。

Abstract: How do we learn to hit a tennis backhand? Not from a thousand hours of tennis tournaments on TV - we work with a coach and practice. We argue this is also the right recipe for teaching dynamic skills to humanoid robots. This follows from a structural property of dynamic skills: the outcome is decided by a short, crucial portion of the trajectory - for a backhand, the ~20cm of racket travel around ball contact. Getting this interaction window right requires coordinating the whole motion, so that control, physics, and morphology act in concert. Learning thus reduces to mastering a handful of distinct actions and, for each, practicing until the window comes out right. To this end, we introduce TaskNPoint, a training protocol which makes the coach-learner division of labor explicit. The human coach contributes four inputs: a discrete set of skills (e.g. different shots), one demonstration per skill, identification of the interaction window, and the goal. Learning in a physically realistic simulation environment fills in each action trajectory and provides robustness to unmodeled events. Crucially, randomized target sampling during training lets a single demonstration generalize zero-shot to unseen goal locations. We test this approach on a Unitree G1 humanoid that hits forehands and backhands against balls thrown by a human, kicks incoming soccer balls, and picks and places boxes from novel locations. We find that learning is successful from short human video demonstrations and under an hour of training on a single GPU, with no per-task reward tuning.

</details>


### [171] [NavIsaacLab: Generating Realistic Crowd via Parallel Robot Learning for Benchmarking Human-aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26265)
*Bingyi Xia,Han Bao,Jingyu Zhu,Hanjing Ye,Yuhan Pang,Guangcheng Chen,Liang Lin,Wenjun Xu,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决人机导航中仿真真实度不足与数据稀缺问题，本文提出了 NavIsaacLab 框架，利用 Isaac Lab 进行大规模并行仿真，结合扩散模型和强化学习实现高保真行人模拟，助力导航策略的训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人机共存导航（Human-aware navigation）研究受限于高质量仿真数据匮乏、行人行为模拟过于简化（依赖手工规则）以及缺乏真实传感器观测模拟，导致导航策略难以在实际复杂环境中应用。

Method: 1. 基于 Isaac Lab 构建物理与视觉仿真环境，支持 GPU 并行加速；2. 结合轨迹扩散模型与对抗性运动学习控制器，实现可控且具物理真实感的行人运动模拟；3. 集成跨尺度场景，用于导航算法的基准测试。

Result: 构建了 NavIsaacLab 框架，实现了兼具照片级视觉反馈与物理真实感的行人交互仿真，支持大规模并行训练，并为最先进的人机导航方法提供了基准测试平台。

Conclusion: NavIsaacLab 提供了一个基于真实物理引擎和照片级渲染的全面框架，填补了高质量、多样化人机共存导航数据缺失的空白，为训练和评估鲁棒的机器人导航策略设定了新基准。

Abstract: Robot autonomous navigation that accounts for surrounding human activities is crucial for ensuring both safety and natural human-robot interaction in real-world environments shared by humans and robots. Simulation of complex and diverse navigation scenarios serves as the foundation for training reliable robot navigation policies and accurately evaluating the performance of algorithms, offering an efficient alternative to manual supervision of real data. However, current human-aware navigation research faces significant challenges due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality scene data. Existing simulation platforms often rely on handcrafted rules to approximate pedestrian behavior and lack the capability to provide extensive sensor signals, typically assuming perfect observations. To address these limitations, this paper presents NavIsaacLab, a comprehensive framework for benchmarking and training human-aware navigation policies through physics-based and photo-realistic simulations of pedestrians and scenes. Based on Isaac Lab, the proposed framework employs photo-realistic scene rendering capabilities and supports parallel simulation on GPU, delivering real-time and accurate 3D visual feedback to robots. To enhance the realism of human behavior, a data-driven approach is employed that incorporates a trajectory diffusion model and an adversarial motion learning controller, enabling controllable, physics-based pedestrian simulation. Furthermore, the integration of diverse cross-scale scenes provides a robust benchmark for state-of-the-art human-aware navigation methods.

</details>


### [172] [Racing a Wheeled Quadruped: Active Load Transfer Mitigation via Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26313)
*Marla Eisman,Brian Lam,Samuel Sonnino,Francesco Borrelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合MPC与RL的分层控制框架，通过主动调整轮式四足机器人的侧倾姿态来减少载荷转移，从而实现了更快的赛道速度与更强的动态稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升轮式四足机器人在高速赛车场景下的侧向稳定性，通过管理侧向载荷转移（LTR）来优化转弯表现及极限速度。

Method: 提出了一种分层控制框架：1. 离线生成时间最优赛车线；2. 在线MPC规划器：基于简化自行车模型，实时最小化侧向载荷转移比（LTR）；3. 低层RL策略：直接控制Unitree Go2-W机器人的16个关节，利用腿部执行器产生侧倾力矩（类似主动悬架）。

Result: 实验表明，该系统使平均LTR降低达44%，最快单圈时间提升8.7%，峰值侧向加速度提升21.3%（达到1.98 $m/s^2$），显著增强了高转速下的行驶稳定性。

Conclusion: 该分层控制框架通过主动侧倾控制有效地提升了四足机器人在高速自动驾驶中的稳定性和性能，证明了结合模型预测控制（MPC）和强化学习（RL）在动态复杂任务中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical control framework using model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning (RL) for active roll control to manage lateral load transfer during autonomous racing of a wheeled quadruped. The framework integrates offline time-optimal raceline generation, an online MPC planner that actively minimizes the lateral Load Transfer Ratio (LTR), and a low-level, whole-body RL policy deployed directly onto the robot's 16 actuators. The MPC is based on a vehicle dynamics bicycle model of the Unitree Go2-W platform. The robot's leg actuators act as active suspension where knee joints generate anti-roll torque to bank into turns. Physical track experiments demonstrate that active roll control reduces mean LTR by up to 44%, improves the fastest lap time by 8.7%, and boosts peak lateral acceleration capability by 21.3% to 1.98 $m/s^2$, maintaining robust high-speed stability beyond the range of a non-tilting baseline controller. Supplementary code and video can be found at https://github.com/meisman-ucb/go2w-roll-control-mpc

</details>


### [173] [Layered Outer-Loop Control for Disturbance-Robust Multi-Waypoint UAV Arrival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26315)
*Runfeng Ling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种分层无人机位置控制架构，通过仿真与硬件平台的两阶段系统评估，解决了学术基准测试与实际部署性能之间的脱节问题，显著提高了无人机在扰动环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管无人机在理想模拟环境下易于实现位置控制，但在真实复杂环境下，如何在保持快速接近表现的同时，实现目标点附近的稳定悬停，并克服单一基准测试带来的过拟合问题，仍是控制领域的一大挑战。

Method: 提出了一种分层终端控制架构（Layered Terminal-Control Architecture），包括三个核心模块：平滑接近路径生成、持久偏置补偿以及受监督的近目标终端调节。通过Phase I（PyBullet随机风场快速筛选）和Phase II（PX4/Gazebo闭环高保真仿真）的两阶段评估，验证架构的可移植性。

Result: 在Phase I中，控制器实现了0.024米的目标点附近平均风扰误差；在Phase II中，通过剔除基准测试先验的转移导向准则，实现了跨场景的性能平衡，并在Tello硬件平台上完成了鲁棒性验证。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过将核心控制器设计与基准测试特有的优化解耦，并辅以跨各种仿真及硬件环境（PyBullet、PX4/Gazebo、Tello无人机）的分层评估，可以显著提升无人机位置控制架构的鲁棒性与可部署性。

Abstract: Disturbance-robust UAV position control is easy to demonstrate in benign simulations but much harder to make fast in approach, well behaved near the target, and credible beyond a single benchmark. This letter presents a layered terminal-control architecture for multi-waypoint UAV position regulation together with a staged evaluation across PyBullet, PX4/Gazebo, and hardware. Phase I uses a PyBullet benchmark with stochastic wind for rapid structural selection, identifying a controller core that separates smooth approach generation, persistent-bias compensation, and supervised near-target terminal regulation. Phase II carries only that main architecture into a more demanding PX4/Gazebo closed loop, where the outer-loop controller acts through a cascaded flight stack with delay-sensitive settling and stronger transit-to-hover coupling. This step exposes which benchmark gains survive autopilot-mediated dynamics and which refinements collapse once the loop becomes more deployment-like. In Phase I, the bare controller attains 0.024 m mean late-stage wind error. In Phase II, the final controller is selected using a transfer-oriented rule emphasizing absence of benchmark priors, cross-scenario balance, and deployable supervisory logic. Strict is used as the primary reporting reference; the supplementary retrospective Grace analysis shows that part of the residual failure set is sensitive to completion semantics rather than gross waypoint-miss behaviour. The evaluation is completed on one Vicon-tracked Tello stack through a two-level hardware study. Taken together, the results suggest that benchmark success becomes more informative when the main controller design is separated from benchmark-specific refinement and remains defensible under harder closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [174] [KRVF: A Source-Aware Semantic Voxel World Representation for Edge Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26321)
*Runfeng Ling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为 KRVF 的移动操作机器人世界表示方法，通过在边缘计算限制下利用任务导向的语义体素，优化了感知数据的管理、深度修复及语义任务查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界建模方法多侧重于全局几何重建，难以满足移动操作机器人对实时性、语义理解的深度需求，且无法在边缘计算限制下有效权衡准确性与可用性。

Method: 提出 KRVF（基于源感知的语义体素世界表示），将局部世界状态编码为包含占用、颜色、语义置信度、时效性和数据源属性的体素；设计了基于深度失效传感的几何/语义分离机制，并实现了地图先验与感知的反馈闭环（用于深度修复）以及任务级语义/抓取查询接口。

Result: 成功实现了基于 ROS 2 的原型系统，能够将在线 RGB-D 数据流转化为任务导向的机器人内存，有效解决了深度传感器缺失导致的几何损坏问题，并支撑了上层任务的可靠查询。

Conclusion: KRVF 是一种适用于边缘计算环境的高效移动操作世界建模方案，通过将任务需求与几何语义分离，实现了对机器人感知数据的有效管理和利用。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators need world models that are current, queryable, semantically meaningful, and usable under edge-compute constraints. This technical report presents KRVF, a source-aware semantic voxel world representation for edge mobile manipulation. Unlike reconstruction-centric mapping pipelines that primarily optimize global geometric fidelity, KRVF represents local world state as task-oriented voxels that encode occupancy, color, semantic evidence, temporal freshness, and evidence source. The representation separates measured occupancy from semantic-prior hypotheses, enabling depth-failure-aware object reasoning without silently corrupting persistent geometry. KRVF also closes a feedback loop between mapping and sensing by rendering map-prior depth for repair, and exposes task-level query operators for semantic objects and grasp candidates. The report formalizes the KRVF representation and documents a ROS 2 implementation that turns online RGB-D observations into a task-facing robot memory.

</details>


### [175] [Scaling Nonlinear Optimization: Many Problems One GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26341)
*John Viljoen,Johanna Haffner,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: jaxipm 是首个基于 JAX 的 GPU 批处理 NLP 求解器，通过重新设计 IPOPT 算法以实现高度并行计算，从而将约束优化任务的求解速度提升了数十倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成熟的 NLP 求解器（如 IPOPT）虽具有强大的约束求解能力和最优性保证，但受限于 CPU 串行处理机制，无法集成到现代 GPU 加速的机器人学习流程中，限制了其在大规模并发任务中的应用。

Method: 提出了基于 JAX 框架的 GPU 批处理非线性规划（NLP）求解器 jaxipm；通过“异构迭代融合”（heterogeneous iteration fusion）消除分支流控制，并采用“迭代级批处理”（iteration level batching）最大程度减少 GPU 空闲时间。

Result: 在四旋翼非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）实验中，jaxipm 的处理吞吐量最高提升了 32.85 倍。

Conclusion: jaxipm 成功实现了将 NLP 求解器从 CPU 迁移至 GPU，通过异构迭代融合和迭代级批处理大幅提升了求解吞吐量，为机器人学提供了高效的并行约束优化工具。

Abstract: Many robotics problems, including trajectory optimization, inverse kinematics, and contact-rich motion planning, reduce to nonlinear programs (NLPs). Mature NLP solvers such as IPOPT can solve these problems, offering hard constraint satisfaction, optimality guarantees, and favorable scaling with problem dimension. These solvers underpin gradient-based methods in robotics, yet remain CPU-bound and solve only one problem at a time, preventing their integration into GPU-batched learning pipelines. On the other hand, sampling-based approaches such as reinforcement learning, model predictive path integral, and imitation learning have become the core of modern robotics research due to their ability to leverage GPU-batched simulators. These simulators can generate orders of magnitude more dynamics rollouts per second than was previously possible. If a GPU-batched NLP solver existed, it would unlock similar speedups in the number of constrained, locally optimal solutions generated per second. This regime of solving many problems concurrently versus solving a single problem at a time is a key requirement for integrating NLP solvers in modern GPU-batched robotics frameworks. To this end, we introduce \texttt{jaxipm}, the first GPU-batched NLP solver, based on IPOPT, and implemented in JAX.
