### [211] [LA4VLA: Learning to Act without Seeing via Language-Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27295)
*Tao Lin,Yuxin Du,Yiran Mao,Zewei Ye,Yilei Zhong,Bing Cheng,Yiming Wang,Jiting Liu,Yang Tian,Junchi Yan,Feiran Wu,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Yuqian Fu,Gen Li,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型因视觉监督过强导致的“语言依赖弱”问题，本文提出了LA4VLA框架，通过引入独立的语言-动作预训练来学习任务无关的技能先验，显著提升了机器人的泛化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在预训练时，密集的视觉-动作监督往往会淹没稀疏的语言-动作信号，导致模型产生视觉捷径（shortcuts），缺乏对语言指令的深度理解，且对视觉变化敏感。

Method: 1. 提出LA4VLA框架：在不依赖视觉观察的情况下学习语言条件动作先验；2. 构建LA4-33K数据集：将专家演示分解为原子动作并配对低级语言描述；3. 提出三种预训练范式：LA-only（仅语言-动作）、顺序式（LA-to-VLA）和混合式（Mixed LA-VLA）预训练。

Result: LA4VLA-1B模型在仿真和真实机器人任务中表现出色，混合预训练使得平均成功率在仿真任务和真实任务中分别提升了高达17.8和45.0个百分点，优于单纯依赖视觉-动作数据的预训练方法。

Conclusion: LA4VLA 被证明是一种有效且互补的预训练策略。通过引入语言-动作先验，该方法显著增强了VLA模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性，混合预训练范式在仿真和真实机器人任务中均取得了优异的性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly pretrained on robot demonstrations by jointly mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, dense visual-action supervision can dominate the comparatively sparse language-action signal. As a result, policies may rely on visual shortcuts rather than learn how language conditions action execution, making them sensitive to visual variations. To address this limitation, we propose LA4VLA, a language-action pretraining framework that enables policies to acquire language-conditioned action priors without visual observations. These priors capture reusable manipulation skills shared across tasks and scenes, reducing reliance on scene-specific visual cues. Specifically, LA4VLA decomposes expert demonstration trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each segment with a corresponding low-level action description. This yields LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K Language-Action (LA) episodes derived entirely from existing demonstrations without additional robot data collection. We further develop LA4VLA-1B, a lightweight 1B-parameter VLA model, and investigate three paradigms for incorporating language-action supervision into VLA learning: LA-only pretraining, sequential LA-to-VLA pretraining, and mixed LA-VLA pretraining. Across simulation and real-world tasks, LA-pretrained policies consistently outperform matched VLA-pretrained counterparts, while combining LA and VLA supervision leads to further gains. In particular, mixed LA-VLA pretraining improves the average success rate of LA4VLA-1B over the no-pretraining baseline by up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage points in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. These results establish LA4VLA as an effective and complementary pretraining strategy for building stronger and more robust VLA policies.

</details>


### [212] [VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27344)
*Yuemin Mao,Uksang Yoo,Jean Oh,Jonathan Francis,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VibeAct 提出了一种通过将压电麦克风捕捉的振动信号转化为抽象的“接触与滑动”物理表示，从而桥接虚拟仿真与现实物理感知的机器人操控新框架，无需高开销的音频信号仿真即可实现高效的灵巧手感知学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人灵巧操作中，接触事件具有高频、局部且易遮挡的特性。虽然压电传感器可捕捉此类信号，但由于这些振动声学信号极难在仿真中高保真建模，导致其难以直接用于端到端的 Sim-to-Real 强化学习任务。

Method: 提出 VibeAct 框架，包含三个核心步骤：1. 在灵巧手上嵌入压电麦克风采集数据；2. 通过回放真实录音在数字孪生中自动标注接触与滑动事件；3. 训练触觉估计器从原始音频预测物理接触状态，并将此抽象表示作为强化学习策略的观测输入。这种解耦方式避免了复杂的原始声学仿真，直接在仿真环境中使用标注的特征进行策略训练。

Result: 在包含重抓取、重定向和插入等五个接触密集型任务中，VibeAct 在仿真环境中的表现全面优于基线方法（仅依赖本体感觉和点云）。在现实物理平台上的部署实验表明，VibeAct 显著提升了任务成功率，且证明了连续滑动幅值信息对反应式控制至关重要。

Conclusion: VibeAct 成功解决了仿生灵巧手在复杂接触任务中难以利用振动触觉信息的难题。通过将录制的触觉特征转化为抽象的接触与滑动表示，该框架有效地简化了感知空间，减小了仿真与现实之间的鸿沟（Sim-to-Real），并显著提升了机器人执行接触密集型任务的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact events that are fast, local, and often visually occluded. Piezoelectric microphones offer a compact and high-bandwidth way to sense these interactions, but the resulting vibro-acoustic signals are difficult to simulate faithfully enough for end-to-end sim-to-real policy learning on dexterous robot hands. We propose VibeAct, a framework that bridges real vibrotactile sensing and simulation-based reinforcement learning through a shared physical representation of contact and slip. In the real world, we embed piezoelectric microphones into a dexterous robot hand and collect vibro-acoustic data through teleoperation, then replay the recordings in a calibrated digital clone to automatically label per-finger contact and slip. A tactile estimator learns to predict contact and slip from real microphone waveforms, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation on the same representation computed directly from simulated contacts. This decoupling lets policies exploit rapid tactile feedback without simulating raw audio. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning regrasping, in-hand reorientation, and insertion, VibeAct consistently outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline in simulation, with the largest gains on tasks requiring sustained reactive control, where the continuous slip-magnitude channel proves the most informative observation. The learned policies transfer to a physical dexterous hand-arm platform, improving success rates on deployed tasks. Project videos and additional details are at https://vibeact.github.io/.

</details>


### [213] [Bridging Performance and Generalization in Reinforcement Learning for Agile Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27348)
*Jonathan Green,Jiaxu Xing,Nico Messikommer,Angel Romero,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶无人机赛车泛化能力差的问题，本文提出了一种基于任务切换和程序化生成的新型强化学习框架，在保持高速竞技性能的同时，实现了跨赛道的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前强化学习无人机赛车算法在面对未知赛道时泛化能力差的问题，克服了现有方法为追求泛化而显著降低飞行速度的局限性。

Method: 提出了一种结合基于“学习进度”的任务感知切换机制（Task-aware switching）与“物理启发”程序化赛道生成器（Physically informed procedural track generator）的强化学习框架。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界测试中，较现有最先进方法实现了7.4倍的泛化性能提升，并在端到端的视觉控制设置中表现出色。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了零样本泛化，在提升无人机鲁棒性的同时未牺牲竞赛速度，弥补了强化学习在敏捷飞行中泛化能力不足的缺陷。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.

</details>


### [214] [Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27353)
*Jiaxu Xing,Zhiyuan Zhu,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yifan Zhai,Rudolf Reiter,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于条件感知动力学模型的持续学习框架，使机器人能够实时识别并适应变化的物理动态，显著提升了复杂动态环境下的任务性能和自适应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人学习控制器通常单次训练后即锁定，无法应对现实世界中风力、负载、电池及硬件磨损等动态变化，缺乏利用部署经验持续改进性能的能力。

Method: 提出一种持续学习框架，结合分析物理先验与神经网络残差来学习条件感知动力学模型；利用循环编码器从最近的交互中推断隐藏条件，并通过可微仿真（differentiable simulation）进行策略学习，实现对重复出现的动态的在线识别与快速自适应。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，该方法能在约1秒内从扰动中恢复，速度比在线残差拟合快5倍，在大扰动下的悬停和跟踪误差分别显著降低了65.7%和53.3%。

Conclusion: 通过在不同隐藏动态环境下的模拟和真实机器人实验证明，该框架能有效适应未观测到的扰动，并在识别和恢复效率上远超现有的在线残差拟合方法。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>


### [215] [RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27355)
*Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Ge Ma,Youran Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RouterVLA证明了通过妥善复用机器人预部署测试数据（并确保评估集的独立性），无需额外参数容量即可显著提升策略路由的可靠性和系统成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队通常会进行大量的策略预部署测试（smoke test），但这些测试数据往往在选择出最优策略后就被废弃。本研究旨在探讨如何高效复用这些历史评估数据来指导策略路由（Policy Selection），以提升系统整体的执行成功率。

Method: 提出了RouterVLA框架，采用“结果不相交的交叉验证”方法：利用一部分历史试用记录（probes）构建各策略的性能画像，使用另一部分独立的试用记录进行策略评估与验证，确保评估集的独立性。

Result: 在34,752条LIBERO-Plus记录上的实验显示，使用基于透明探查结果的路由规则，使测试集的成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp）。研究同时发现，未进行结果分离会导致测量增益虚高（约1.87倍）。

Conclusion: 通过在评估中引入“结果不相交的交叉验证”（outcome-disjoint cross-fitting），可以有效复用预部署评估数据来优化策略路由，并避免评估偏差。单纯的模型扩展能提升单个策略表现，但利用“系统配置感知”（commissioning-aware）的路由机制能显著提升整体系统性能。

Abstract: We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.

</details>


### [216] [World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27374)
*Manish Kumar Govind,Dominick Reilly,Smit Patel,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REGEN 利用世界动作模型（WAMs）动态生成虚拟训练数据，使机器人能够在不保留原始演示的情况下持续学习新任务，有效克服了灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续机器人模仿学习中，存储所有原始演示数据既不切实际又存在隐私问题，而现有方法往往面临灾难性遗忘，需要一种能够在不依赖原始数据的情况下实现任务复现的机制。

Method: 提出 REGEN 框架：利用 WAM 递归合成伪回放轨迹，在持续学习过程中通过这些合成数据训练策略，从而替代原始人类演示，实现任务回放。

Result: 在仿真和真实操作实验中，REGEN 相比序列微调减少了高达 50% 的灾难性遗忘，且效果接近需要存储真实演示数据的经验回放（Experience Replay）方法。

Conclusion: 基于世界动作模型（WAMs）的递归生成回放（REGEN）为持续机器人模仿学习提供了一种无需存储原始演示数据的高效途径，显著减轻了灾难性遗忘。研究明确了长时视觉退化和动作-观测不一致是当前技术的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>


### [217] [Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27375)
*Arthur Allshire,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Ritvik Singh,Adam Rashid,Hongsuk Choi,David McAllister,Justin Yu,Yiyuan Chen,Huang Huang,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Rocky Duan,Phillip Isola,Jitendra Malik,Fred Shentu,Guanya Shi,Philipp Wu,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABC是一个全开源的机器人操作行为克隆生态，包含迄今最大的130K轨迹数据集、硬件设计及协同训练方案，旨在通过标准化的工具链提升机器人研究的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作领域缺乏大规模、高可复现且完全开源的数据集与基础设施，限制了研究者在行为克隆方向的公平竞争与快速迭代。

Method: 提出了基于行为克隆（Behavior Cloning）的完整开源栈ABC，包括：构建包含3500小时/13万个任务轨迹的teleop数据集（ABC-130K）；提供配套的硬件、训练设施及仿真流水线；通过对比Diffusion Transformers (DiT) 和 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型的架构选择，建立了一套仿真与现实数据协同训练的评估方案。

Result: 在包括盒子折叠、从钱包中取出信用卡等复杂灵巧操作任务中表现优异；证实了仿真数据作为真实世界模型训练代理的可行性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过提供完整、可复现的开源工具包，作者旨在降低机器人行为克隆的研究门槛，推动社区在标准基准下共同进步。

Abstract: We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Physics-guided Convolutional Neural Network for Domain Growth Prediction in Systems with Conserved Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26128)
*Vijay Yadav,Madhu Priya,Manish Dev Shrimali,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创新的物理引导注意力卷积神经网络，不仅能高效模拟复杂的相分离动力学，还实现了长期演化的物理一致性与高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值解法在处理非线性偏微分方程（PDEs）时计算成本高昂，需要开发高效的深度学习代理模型来模拟物理、化学和生物系统的时空演化。

Method: 提出了一种结合注意力机制（Attention-based）与物理引导（Physics-guided）的卷积神经网络（CNN）代理模型，用于学习和预测系统的时空演化。

Result: 模型在长程推演中保持了高精度与稳定性；成功模拟了临界与非临界混合物的相分离；在演化过程中严格守恒混合物组分；准确捕捉了域尺寸（Domain size）的增长规律，并符合Lifshitz-Slyozov定律。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络能够高效、准确地模拟遵循守恒动力学的复杂系统（如Cahn-Hilliard方程描述的相分离过程），具备良好的长期稳定性和物理一致性。

Abstract: The spatiotemporal evolution of many physical, chemical, and biological systems is described by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep neural network-based surrogate models have gained increasing interest as efficient alternatives to computationally expensive traditional numerical solvers. In this work, we propose an attention-based, physics-guided convolutional neural network as a surrogate model to learn the microstructural evolution of such systems. We train the model to accurately predict the full time-evolution of phase separation in binary mixtures governed by the Cahn-Hilliard equation. We show that predictions from our trained surrogate model remain stable and accurate over long-time rollouts for both critical and off-critical mixtures and preserve the mixture composition throughout evolution. We also show that our model accurately captures the growth of domain size and is consistent with the Lifshitz-Slyozov domain-growth law. The prediction results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for modeling systems with conserved kinetics and can be extended to other complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [219] [\chisao{}: A GPU-Native Parallel Optimizer for Multimodal Black-Box Functions via Convergence-Anticonvergence Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26164)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHISAO 是一种专为 GPU 并行计算设计的新型黑盒优化算法，通过独特的震荡循环机制和模式锁定策略，在实现 100% 模式恢复的同时，较传统序列算法在高维和复杂环境下表现出极高的速度优势和稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态黑盒优化算法（如 basin-hopping, CMA-ES 等）多为序列化计算，难以利用现代 GPU 的大规模并行能力，且在高维空间中容易陷入局部陷阱，导致难以全局收敛。

Method: 提出了 CHISAO (Convergence-Halt-Invert-Stick-And-Oscillate) 算法，这是一种 GPU 原生的种群优化器。该方法引入了“收敛-反收敛”震荡循环来逃离局部极小值，同时锁定已确认的模式；通过“Repulse Monkey”和“Golden Rooster”两种策略进行自适应重采样以维持多样性；完全依赖无导数方法（有限差分）进行 GPU 并行计算。

Result: 在 SFU 基准套件中，CHISAO 在所有 42 个函数测试中实现了 100% 的模式恢复；在高维（d≥8）条件下全面超越 CPU 基准；相比 basin-hopping 在特定函数上获得最高 34 倍的加速，在单峰函数上最高达 39 倍；在存在显著噪声（σ=1.0）的环境下仍保持 100% 的可靠性。

Conclusion: CHISAO 是一种高效、鲁棒且高度并行的多模态黑盒函数寻优算法，在处理高维复杂优化问题时优于传统的序列化方法，且具备极强的噪声容忍度，实现了从 CPU 串行到 GPU 并行优化的范式转变。

Abstract: Finding all modes of a multimodal black-box function is a fundamental challenge in optimization, Bayesian inference, and scientific computing. Existing approaches -- basin-hopping, CMA-ES, multistart gradient descent -- operate sequentially and cannot exploit the massive parallelism of modern GPU hardware. We introduce \chisao{} (\textbf{C}onvergence-\textbf{H}alt-\textbf{I}nvert-\textbf{S}tick-\textbf{A}nd-\textbf{O}scillate), a GPU-native population optimizer that runs an entire sample batch simultaneously and exploits a deliberate convergence-anticonvergence oscillation cycle to escape local traps while freezing confirmed modes. The structural move is asymmetric: samples that reach true peaks are frozen (``stuck'') and preserved, while the rest keep exploring via momentum-based anti-convergence and stochastically smoothed gradients. Adaptive reseeding via two complementary strategies (Repulse Monkey and Golden Rooster) maintains population diversity throughout. On all 42 functions of the Simon Fraser University optimization benchmark suite across dimensions $d \in \{2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64\}$, \chisao{} achieves \textbf{100\%} mode recovery where all CPU baselines collapse at $d \geq 8$ on the hardest multimodal functions, at up to \textbf{$34\times$} speedup over basin-hopping on functions where all methods succeed (Michalewicz $d=64$) and up to \textbf{$39\times$} on unimodal functions (Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid $d=64$, pure GPU dividend). All benchmarks evaluate the objective by value alone -- gradients come from finite differences -- so the reported speedups are a derivative-free worst case. Under substantial likelihood noise ($σ_{\mathrm{noise}}$ up to 1.0), mode detection remains 100\% reliable. The algorithm is available as a standalone open-source Python package on PyPI.

</details>


### [220] [Implementation of reinforcement learning in chemical reaction networks: application to phototaxis as curiosity-driven exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26168)
*Ruyi Tang,Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Colliaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于POMDP的生物建模框架，证明单细胞生物（如衣藻）执行的“游动-翻滚”行为实质上是一种主动的信息采集机制，旨在通过动态采样克服感官环境的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单细胞趋光性模型多将其视为受刺激后的简单机械反应（run-tumble），忽略了细胞在面对噪声和不完整环境信号时，如何通过主动采样来降低感官歧义（最小化认知视角）。

Method: 1. 建立部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）框架，结合生化反应速率方程（CRN-ODEs）模拟细胞内部状态更新。
2. 利用逆向强化学习（IRL）对30条衣藻（Chlamydomonas）运动轨迹进行分析，推断其行为目标。
3. 采用随机模拟算法（SSA）进行基准测试，验证模型表现。

Result: 模型成功复现了实验观察到的衣藻趋光对齐分布；证明了通过调节翻滚行为，细胞能有效采样环境以消除传感器噪声，其表现与现有的随机模拟基准相当。

Conclusion: 研究表明游动-翻滚（run-tumble）运动并非纯粹的机械过程，而是一种为了降低传感器歧义、主动获取信息的适应性策略。细胞内的生物化学网络能够通过简单的推断机制支持此类复杂的信息导向行为。

Abstract: Living systems navigate environments using noisy and incomplete sensory signals. In unicellular algae, phototaxis is often modeled as a mechanistic run--tumble process driven by stimulus--response rules. However, such descriptions overlook how organisms actively sample their environment to reduce sensory ambiguity. From a minimal cognition perspective, we reframe this navigation as a subjective, information-driven sensorimotor process. To this end, we propose a framework linking a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with biochemical reaction dynamics. Environmental variables are hidden, while the cell updates a minimal internal state from each observation through a memoryless Bayesian step. These internal dynamics balance orienting toward light with exploratory reorientation and can be implemented through Chemical-Reaction-Network Ordinary Differential Equations (CRN--ODEs). Our model includes a biophysical observation process for photoreception and a chemically computable polynomial bound on information gain. Using Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) on 30 experimentally recorded Chlamydomonas trajectories, we infer the behavioral objective consistent with observed phototactic motion and benchmark the resulting dynamics with standard Stochastic Simulation Algorithm (SSA) baselines. Our model reproduces the empirical alignment-to-light distribution, comparable to objective SSA baselines on this dataset. Within this framework, run--tumble alternation emerges as an information-acquisition strategy: tumbling reorients the cell to sample new sensory configurations and resolve sensor ambiguity, demonstrating how intracellular biochemical networks can support adaptive information-seeking behavior in cellular navigation.

</details>


### [221] [Statistical and Structural Approaches to Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26200)
*Antonio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判了传统算法公平性研究过度依赖点估计和忽略社会结构语境的局限性，旨在通过反思这些基础缺陷，为构建更符合复杂社会技术环境的公平性框架提供理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习系统已演变为复杂的社会技术架构，直接影响人类机会分配，但现有算法公平性研究在面对结构性不平等时，往往受限于过于简化的模型（如依赖点估计预测、忽略个体所处社会背景），导致干预措施效果不佳。

Method: 本文采取的是一种批判性的理论分析方法，通过审视当前算法公平性领域在应对复杂社会技术架构时的失效原因，对现有的技术范式（如确定性评估和个体化分析）进行解构。

Result: 明确界定了当前算法公平性研究的两个核心缺陷：第一，过度依赖确定性的点估计（deterministic point estimates）进行审计；第二，将个体视为脱离社会结构语境的孤立实体分析。

Conclusion: 指出当前算法公平性研究范式存在的局限性（确定性点估计和缺乏结构性语境），并以此作为构建更稳健、更具社会技术语境的公平性方法的立足点。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems have outgrown their origins as isolated predictive constructs, evolving into complex socio-technical architectures that actively mediate human opportunity. As algorithms increasingly determine access to economic and social opportunities, it has become widely recognized that these systems are deeply embedded with the structural inequalities and prejudices of their environments. The field of algorithmic fairness emerged in response to the growing recognition that models optimized for predictive accuracy can systematically disadvantage marginalized groups. Early mitigation strategies, however, rested on fragile simplifications that limited their effectiveness in complex socio-technical environments. This thesis identifies and addresses two fundamental limitations of contemporary fairness paradigms: the reliance on deterministic point estimates for auditing and the treatment of individuals as isolated entities devoid of structural context.

</details>


### [222] [Neural Architecture Search for Generative Adversarial Networks: A Comprehensive Review and Critical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26169)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了NAS在GAN设计中的应用，通过分类比较现有方法，总结了其在提升GAN性能方面的优势与挑战，并为未来的研究方向提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动设计GAN架构具有挑战性，而NAS能自动化设计过程并优化GAN架构，因此有必要对该领域的方法进行全面梳理与评价。

Method: 对现有的神经架构搜索（NAS）应用于对抗生成网络（GAN）的方法进行了系统性综述、分类和比较，分析了不同的搜索策略、评价指标及性能表现。

Result: 研究发现进化算法和基于梯度的方法在特定情况下表现出优越性；强调了现有评价指标的局限性，并指出了数据集多样性对于评估GAN性能的关键作用。

Conclusion: NAS能有效提升GAN的性能、稳定性和效率。未来的研究需要关注评价指标的改进（超越IS和FID）、在更多样化的数据集上进行评估，并进一步优化搜索策略（如进化算法和梯度法）。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a pivotal technique in optimizing the design of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), automating the search for effective architectures while addressing the challenges inherent in manual design. This paper provides a comprehensive review of NAS methods applied to GANs, categorizing and comparing various approaches based on criteria such as search strategies, evaluation metrics, and performance outcomes. The review highlights the benefits of NAS in improving GAN performance, stability, and efficiency, while also identifying limitations and areas for future research. Key findings include the superiority of evolutionary algorithms and gradient-based methods in certain contexts, the importance of robust evaluation metrics beyond traditional scores like Inception Score (IS) and Fréchet Inception Distance (FID), and the need for diverse datasets in assessing GAN performance. By presenting a structured comparison of existing NAS-GAN techniques, this paper aims to guide researchers in developing more effective NAS methods and advancing the field of GANs.

</details>


### [223] [Learning Probabilistic Filters with Strictly Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26497)
*Eviatar Bach,Ricardo Baptista,Jochen Bröcker,Bohan Chen,Andrew Stuart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 PSEF，这是一种利用 Transformer 和适当评分规则进行训练的集数据同化方法，无需预先存在的真实标签，即可通过合成轨迹学习复杂的非高斯贝叶斯滤波分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯滤波分布是量化动力系统不确定性的核心，但通常难以获得监督学习目标。现有方法大多依赖于高斯假设或简单的均方误差（MSE）准则，无法有效捕捉复杂的非高斯、多模态后验分布。

Method: 提出了一种基于转换器（Transformer）的“适当评分集滤波”（PSEF）方法。该方法将分析步骤表示为置换不变的映射，直接输入预测集和观测值，通过最小化能量分数（Energy Score，一种严格适当的评分规则）来训练模型，以近似贝叶斯滤波分布。

Result: 数学证明了在可实现性假设下，总体目标函数由真实的贝叶斯滤波分布最小化；数值实验表明该方法能准确近似复杂的系统分布，且在数据同化任务中表现出比 MSE 驱动的机器学习方法及经典方法更强的性能。

Conclusion: PSEF 证明了通过适当的评分规则（Proper Scoring Rules）可以有效地从合成数据中学习复杂的贝叶斯滤波分布。其表现优于传统的基于 MSE 的学习方法和经典数据同化方法，尤其在处理高度非高斯、多模态后验分布时展现了显著优势。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering of partially and noisily observed dynamical systems seeks to infer the evolving conditional distribution of the state of a dynamical system, given observations, in an online fashion. This Bayesian filtering distribution is the natural object for uncertainty quantification, but it is rarely available as a supervised learning target. However, one can often use the forecast model to generate synthetic system trajectories, along with synthetic observations. We introduce the proper scoring ensemble filter (PSEF), an ensemble data assimilation method based on training an analysis map to approximate the filtering distribution using only synthetic state--observation trajectories. The analysis step is represented as a permutation-invariant, transformer-based map that takes as input a forecast ensemble and observations, producing an analysis ensemble. Training is based on strictly proper scoring rules -- with the energy score used in our implementation -- so that probabilistic accuracy is rewarded over the whole probability distribution. We prove that, under a realizability assumption, the population objective is minimized by the true Bayesian filtering distribution. We also derive the finite-ensemble empirical objective used in training and relate its single state--observation trajectory form to the population objective, using a mean-field consistency argument. Numerical experiments show that the learned filter accurately approximates challenging filtering distributions, including nonlinear, non-Gaussian, and multi-modal posteriors, and achieves stronger performance in data assimilation tasks than classical methods or learning-based methods with mean-squared-error objectives. For close-to-Gaussian problems, learning a correction to the EnKF is the best approach, while for highly non-Gaussian problems an end-to-end approach that discards this inductive bias is superior.

</details>


### [224] [Finding Stationary Points by Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27082)
*Helin Wang,Chenyi Zhang,Xiwen Tao,Yexin Zhang,Tongyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅基于比较预言的非凸函数平稳点搜索问题，在经典设置下实现了$\widetilde O(n^2/\epsilon^{1.5})$的查询复杂度，并提出了首个量子算法，将查询复杂度提升至$\widetilde O(n/\epsilon^{1.5})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅能获取比较信息（即判断哪点函数值更大）而无法直接获取函数值或梯度信息的非凸优化场景下，探究如何高效寻找函数的平稳点。

Method: 1. 经典算法：通过子程序对归一化Hessian进行$\delta$精度估计，结合梯度下降思想寻找$\epsilon$-平稳点。\n2. 量子算法：利用量子叠加态的特性处理比较预言（comparison oracle），加速Hessian估计过程。

Result: 1. 经典算法达到$\widetilde O(n^2/\epsilon^{1.5})$的查询复杂度。\n2. 量子算法达到$\widetilde O(n/\epsilon^{1.5})$的查询复杂度，实现了相对于经典算法的加速。

Conclusion: 本文为仅有比较信息的非凸函数优化提供了理论框架。在经典模型下，提出了一种利用归一化Hessian估计的算法，复杂度为$\widetilde O(n^2/\epsilon^{1.5})$；在量子模型下，通过量子叠加查询，将查询复杂度进一步优化至$\widetilde O(n/\epsilon^{1.5})$。

Abstract: We study the problem of finding stationary points of non-convex functions when access to the objective is provided only through a comparison oracle that, given two points, outputs which has the larger function value. For a twice differentiable $f\colon\mathbb R^n\to\mathbb R$ with Lipschitz gradient and Hessian, we develop an algorithm that visits an $ε$-stationary point using $\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$ queries. Our approach uses a subroutine that estimates the normalized Hessian to accuracy $δ$ using $\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$ queries. We further study this problem with a quantum comparison oracle model where queries can be made in superpositions, and develop the first quantum algorithm that finds an $ε$-stationary point, which takes $\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$ queries.

</details>


### [225] [KG-TRACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Mechanistic Grounding in Antimicrobial Resistance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26179)
*Naman Garg,Sarika Jain,Sourav Yadav,Bharat K. Bhargava,Ghanapriya Singh,Abhishek Srivastava,Parimal Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KG-TRACE 是一种新型神经符号框架，通过将 WHO 生物知识图谱嵌入耐药性预测模型，在保持高准确率的同时实现了预测过程的生物学溯源，提升了模型对临床决策的可解释性与信任度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于全基因组测序（WGS）的抗微生物药物耐药性（AMR）预测模型虽然准确，但缺乏与已知生物学通路的逻辑关联，导致“黑盒”问题，限制了其在临床环境中的可信度与审计能力。

Method: 提出一种神经符号框架（KG-TRACE），利用 WHO 突变知识图谱（KG）作为约束；采用 RotatE 模型生成 KG 嵌入；引入“认知信任门”（epistemic trust gate）动态平衡神经特征与符号化生物知识；开发了生物接地比率（BGR）指标用于量化模型与生物学的对齐程度。

Result: 在 CRyPTIC M. tuberculosis 数据集上，对异烟肼的预测 AUROC 达到 0.9760；实现了 92.5% 的异烟肼耐药预测符号化覆盖率；能够有效识别耐多药（MDR）共现伪影并标记不确定案例。

Conclusion: 通过将神经模型与生物知识图谱结合，KG-TRACE 实现了高可解释性的耐药性预测，不仅保证了预测精度，还为临床决策提供了可验证的审计追踪，弥合了预测准确性与临床信任之间的差距。

Abstract: While WGS-based AMR prediction has reached high accuracy, existing models lack a mechanism to ground neural attributions in established biological pathways. We present KG-TRACE, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that integrates the WHO mutation knowledge graph (KG) as a structured biological constraint on a neural genomic model. Unlike existing methods that learn statistical patterns in isolation, KG-TRACE fuses genomic features and RotatE-based KG embeddings through a learned epistemic trust gate, dynamically weighting neural evidence against symbolic biological knowledge.
  Evaluated on the CRyPTIC M. tuberculosis cohort, KG-TRACE achieves an AUROC of 0.9760 for isoniazid, achieving competitive accuracy while its primary value lies in symbolic grounding, not predictive uplift. More importantly, we introduce the Biological Grounding Ratio (BGR), a dataset-level metric that quantifies alignment between neural attributions and established biology. Our framework achieves a 92.5% symbolic coverage of isoniazid-resistant predictions and effectively identifies MDR co-occurrence artifacts by issuing laboratory follow-up flags for 'UNCERTAIN' cases. We demonstrate that neuro-symbolic grounding provides a verifiable audit trail for clinicians, bridging the gap between predictive accuracy and clinical trust.

</details>


### [226] [Escaping Iterative Parameter-Space Noise: Differentially Private Learning with a Hypernetwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26772)
*Naoki Nishikawa,Shokichi Takakura,Satoshi Hasegawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于超网络的隐私保护学习框架，通过在低维数据嵌入而非高维参数空间注入噪声，有效降低了隐私保护训练对模型性能的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的DP-SGD方法需要在训练过程中反复在参数空间注入大规模高维噪声，这极大地限制了神经网络的训练效果和性能。

Method: 提出一种基于超网络（Hypernetwork）的DP学习框架：1.利用预训练超网络将私有数据映射为模型参数；2.通过将个体数据嵌入聚合并进行差分隐私扰动，获取低维数据集的DP嵌入；3.超网络根据该嵌入直接生成目标模型参数，避免了参数空间内的迭代噪声注入。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法在合成设置下优于DP-SGD；在扩散模型的LoRA微调任务中，该方法实现了更低的FID评分，优于DP-SGD及其他现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的DP学习框架，通过在低维数据集嵌入上注入噪声而非在模型空间注入梯度噪声，证明了在固定隐私预算下能显著提升模型效用，并在生成式模型微调任务中优于现有DP-SGD方法。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) training of neural networks is often hindered by the large amount of noise required by gradient-based methods such as DP-SGD, which repeatedly inject high-dimensional noise in parameter space throughout training. In this paper, we propose a new framework for DP learning that avoids iterative optimization in parameter space. Instead of updating the target model using privatized gradients, we employ a hypernetwork trained on public datasets to map a private dataset to the parameters of the target model. Specifically, each example is embedded into a low-dimensional representation, the embeddings are aggregated and perturbed to obtain a DP dataset embedding, and the hypernetwork generates the target model parameters from this noisy embedding. Because privacy noise is injected only once into a low-dimensional dataset representation, our approach can significantly reduce the adverse effect of noise. We theoretically show in a synthetic setting that, under a fixed privacy budget, models produced by our approach achieve higher utility than those trained with DP-SGD. Moreover, we apply our approach to LoRA fine-tuning of diffusion models and show that it achieves lower FID than LoRA models trained with DP-SGD and other public-data-guided methods.

</details>


### [227] [Necessary but Not Sufficient: Temperature Control and Reproducibility in LLM-as-Judge Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26185)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM作为评估器并不总是确定性的。即使设置了温度为0，评分仍可能出现不可复现的波动，评估工具应将“评分者分歧”视为评估结果的一部分，而非仅提供单一得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前LLM-as-a-judge已成为安全评估的标准，人们普遍假设将采样温度（temperature）设为0即可获得确定性结果，但这一假设在实际工程实践中缺乏验证。

Method: 研究人员对Japan AISI的开源安全评估工具aisev进行了测试，跨越了2个API提供商、3个模型层级和5种采样配置（包括默认温度和被强制为0的温度），共运行了690次API调用以观察评分的一致性和复现性。

Result: 1. 在许多评估工具中因未显式设置温度，导致调用默认为1.0，造成高达50%的评分不一致；2. 即使强制设为0，由于模型机制原因（如Claude Opus的变动），边缘用例依然存在无法复现评分的情况。

Conclusion: LLM作为评判者（LLM-as-a-judge）存在根本性的不确定性，即便在理论上的确定性设置下也无法消除。现有的评估工具如果不提供方差或评分者分歧指标，可能会将噪声误报为安全属性。建议将评分者分歧作为核心评估指标。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge ("grader") components are now standard in evaluation harnesses, including safety evaluations where a pass/fail verdict may gate downstream deployment decisions. A widespread assumption is that setting the grader's sampling temperature to 0 makes grading deterministic. We test this assumption against a real safety-evaluation codebase (Japan AISI's open-source aisev) and show it fails on two levels. First, the harness invokes its grader without setting temperature or seed; the underlying provider silently applies its default of 1.0, so items near the decision boundary flip pass/fail across identical runs (per-item disagreement up to ~50% over 20 runs). Second, pinning temperature=0 reduces but does not eliminate flips: across 690 API calls spanning two providers, three model tiers, and five sampling configurations, 1-2 of 7 borderline items remain non-reproducible even under forced greedy decoding (top_k=1). Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 has since deprecated temperature entirely, rendering the primary mitigation inapplicable to newer model generations. These findings expose a structural gap: evaluation harnesses that report single-run verdicts without variance or grader-disagreement metrics can present noise as a safety property. We release a reproduction harness (690 calls, 7 conditions) and recommend that harnesses treat grader disagreement as a first-class health metric alongside the scores themselves.

</details>


### [228] [Asymptotically Optimal Learning for Parametric Prophet Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26893)
*Jung-hun Kim,Anna Grebennikova,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有未知参数的指数族分布下的先知不等式学习问题，提出了一种基于置信区间的动态规划算法，有效实现了在线学习下的最优渐近竞争比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先知不等式（prophet inequalities）中，学习环境往往是未知的。本文旨在探讨当奖励服从具有未知参数θ的指数族分布时，如何在缺乏离线样本的情况下，仅通过在线观察实现最优的渐近竞争比。

Method: 1. 通过理论分析刻画了指数族分布下先知不等式的最优完全信息渐近竞争比；
2. 设计了一种基于置信区间（confidence-based）的动态规划策略，利用分布的参数化结构处理在线学习问题；
3. 导出了具体分布的收敛速率，并通过数值实验验证了算法性能。

Result: 1. 成功刻画了指数族分布在无界和有界支撑条件下的最优渐近竞争比；
2. 提出的在线算法在不需要外部先验知识的情况下，达到了理论上的最优渐近竞争比；
3. 提供了具体分布的收敛速率分析及数值验证结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用指数族参数的显式结构，可以在无需离线样本的情况下，仅通过在线观察实现最优的渐近竞争比。此外，对于无界支撑情况，得出了明确的渐近竞争比极限公式；对于有界支撑情况，该极限为1。

Abstract: We study learning in prophet inequalities with i.i.d. rewards drawn from an exponential-type parametric family with an unknown parameter $θ$, a class that includes exponential, Pareto, and bounded-support power-family distributions. We first characterize the optimal full-information asymptotic competitive ratio for this family. In the unbounded-support case, the limit is $ {\left(θ/({θ-c_+})\right)^{c_+/θ}}/ {Γ(1-c_+/θ)},$ while in the bounded-support case, the limit is $1$. We then propose a confidence-based dynamic-programming policy for online learning. By exploiting the explicit parametric structure, the policy achieves the same optimal asymptotic competitive ratio using only online observations, without external offline samples. We further derive distribution-specific convergence rates for canonical examples. Finally, numerical experiments on synthetic instances illustrate the performance of our algorithm.

</details>


### [229] [Clue-Guided Money Laundering Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26189)
*Boyang Wang,Jianing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有反洗钱方法与实际调查流程脱节的问题，本文提出了 Clue2Group 框架，通过引入局部上下文构建、多语义时空 GNN 以及多维度证据融合，实现了从初始线索出发的洗钱团伙逐步精确发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图异常检测（节点级）和全局团伙发现（整个网络搜索）方法无法匹配实际反洗钱（AML）调查工作流，实际调查往往从特定线索出发并逐步扩展。现存方法缺乏这种交互式、线索驱动的调查能力。

Method: 提出了 Clue2Group 框架：1) 构建紧凑的局部调查上下文以消除噪声并保留洗钱结构特征；2) 利用多语义时空图神经网络（multi-semantic local-temporal GNN）估计线索条件下的局部风险场；3) 综合风险、结构及先验模式证据，实现洗钱团伙的逐步恢复。

Result: 在两个大规模 AML 基准数据集上的实验表明，Clue2Group 能有效处理线索驱动的调查，在恢复完整洗钱结构方面表现出优于现有方法的实用性。

Conclusion: Clue2Group 框架成功地将基于图的 AML 研究与实际调查流程结合，提供了一种可行的、基于线索驱动的洗钱团伙发现方案，显著提升了洗钱团伙识别的针对性和有效性。

Abstract: Money Laundering Group Discovery (MLGD) aims to identify hidden criminal groups and recover their complete structures in large-scale financial networks. Existing graph anomaly detection methods mainly produce node-level risk alerts, while global group discovery methods passively search for suspicious groups over the whole network. Both are mismatched with real Anti-money-laundering (AML) investigations, where analysts usually start from a concrete clue and gradually expand the investigation to recover the responsible group. To address this gap, we propose Clue-Guided Group Discovery (CGGD), where a laundering group is progressively recovered from an initial clue set through analyst interaction. We further propose Clue2Group, a framework that first constructs a compact local investigation context to reduce noise and preserve chain-like and cycle-like laundering structures. It then estimates a clue-conditioned local risk field with a multi-semantic local-temporal GNN, and finally integrates risk, structural, and prior-pattern evidence to recover a coherent laundering group. Experiments on two large-scale AML benchmarks show that Clue2Group provides a practical clue-driven analysis framework for AML investigations, offering a feasible step toward bridging the gap between graph-based AML research and real investigation workflows.

</details>


### [230] [Decision-Aligned Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26990)
*Annika Schneider,Tommy Rochussen,Joshua Stiller,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出常规机器学习不确定性测度与下游决策效用脱钩，并提出了“决策对齐”评估框架及新的“先验加权效用指标”来校正这一偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习不确定性指标（如NLL、ECE）在评估时未考虑下游决策任务的具体需求，导致指标性能高并不等同于决策效用好，存在严重的指标与决策目标错配问题。

Method: 提出了“决策对齐（decision-alignment）”评估标准，并基于此设计了一类新的“先验加权效用指标（prior-weighted utility metrics）”，这是一种特殊的真评分规则（proper scoring rules）。

Result: 实验表明，常规UQ指标在许多决策任务中存在偏差或误导，而研究提出的新指标在各项基准测试和真实案例中均能与决策效用保持高度一致。

Conclusion: 现有的不确定性量化（UQ）评估指标往往与下游任务的实际决策效用脱节。研究提出的“先验加权效用指标”能有效对齐决策需求，为UQ评估提供了一种更具原则性与实用性的替代方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimates in machine learning are typically evaluated using generic metrics such as the negative log-likelihood and expected calibration error, yet good performance on such metrics does not necessarily imply high utility in downstream decisions. We introduce decision-alignment, a criterion that reveals which evaluation metrics meaningfully align with downstream utilities. Applying this framework, we show that many widely used uncertainty metrics are either misaligned with common decision problems or encode pathological prior beliefs about the downstream task. We then propose prior-weighted utility metrics, a special class of proper scoring rules that provides decision-aligned uncertainty evaluation. Across benchmark experiments and real-world case studies, our metrics consistently align with realized decision utility, while conventional metrics do not. Our results surface flaws in the current UQ evaluation protocol and offer a principled extension of existing metrics toward decision-relevant UQ evaluation.

</details>


### [231] [Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26192)
*Jialan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 FHPLF 模型，通过二进制梯度传输、投影汉明距离和新型隐私增强策略，解决了联邦环境中哈希学习在精度、通信效率和隐私保护之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈希学习方法存在数据中心化隐私风险；而现有的联邦学习方法又存在通信开销大、隐私泄露风险以及二进制码表达能力受限等问题。

Method: 提出了联邦哈希投影潜在因子（FHPLF）模型，包含三个核心创新：1. 使用二进制梯度矩阵替代实值矩阵；2. 采用投影汉明距离（Projected Hamming Distance）提升哈希码的表达能力；3. 设计了安全二进制梯度重组与隐私增强上传（SBG-PEU）策略。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验结果表明，FHPLF 在精度、效率和隐私保护方面均优于现有的先进哈希学习和联邦学习方法。

Conclusion: FHPLF 在保证隐私、通信效率和模型精度的三者之间取得了良好的平衡，是解决去中心化哈希学习中性能权衡的有效方案。

Abstract: Hash Learning (HL) is an efficient representation learning approach that maps real-valued data into compact binary representations. Traditional HL methods typically require users to upload personal data to a central server, which is incompatible with increasingly stringent data security regulations. Federated Learning (FL) provides a decentralized paradigm for learning globally optimal models without centralizing private data. However, most FL methods rely on transmitting large-scale real-valued gradient information, leading to high communication overhead and potential privacy risks. Integrating HL into FL is a promising solution. Nevertheless, existing HL methods suffer from limited representational capacity of binary codes, which may degrade model accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose a Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor (FHPLF) model. FHPLF introduces three key innovations: (a) replacing real-valued gradient matrices with binary gradient-like matrices, significantly reducing computation, storage, and communication costs while enhancing privacy protection; (b) leveraging Projected Hamming Distance for similarity modeling, which captures the importance of individual binary bits to improve representation capability; and (c) proposing a Secure Binary Gradient Reassembly and Privacy-Enhanced Upload (SBG-PEU) strategy to further reduce the risk of user interaction leakage during transmission. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that FHPLF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HL and FL methods, achieving a favorable trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and privacy preservation.

</details>


### [232] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对共享词表的大模型，提出了一种高效的无训练源选择方法 FisherSketch。它通过流式估算头部的 Fisher 对齐信息，有效替代了计算昂贵的传统度量，为模型迁移和任务诊断提供了一个紧凑且信息丰富的新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型（LLM）的科学领域（如分子、蛋白质序列），当候选语料库共享分词器但预测目标不同时，现有的表示相似度度量（如 CKA）在预测迁移能力上失效，且传统的基于更新几何的度量（如 Fisher 信息矩阵）因在大词表规模下计算开销过大而不可行。

Method: 提出了 FisherSketch 算法，利用共享输出头设置下的 Fisher 对齐特性，将其转化为联合激活-误差空间中的核均值嵌入余弦值。该方法仅需单次流式处理即可计算，并通过极小的任务签名（16 KB）实现高效的估计，避免了显式构建大规模 Fisher 矩阵。

Result: FisherSketch 在保证计算效率和极低内存占用的前提下，能够有效识别模型权重更新的相似性。实验表明，该方法在区分任务方面优于传统的激活相似度度量，并成功应用于 Llama-3.1-8B 的验证实验，能够精准捕获驱动任务相似性的底层因素。

Conclusion: FisherSketch 提供了一种高效、内存友好且准确的无训练源选择方法，克服了传统表示度量在共享词表模型中失效的问题，不仅可用于源选择，还能有效诊断 LLM 任务间的相似性来源。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [233] [Topology-Informed Neural Networks for Flood Detection in Optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26204)
*Sophia Li,Max Zhao,Raghu G. Raj,Tianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入拓扑数据分析（TDA）提取洪水图像的几何特征，并将其融入深度学习模型，在提升洪水检测准确性的同时改善了黑盒模型的不可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有洪水检测模型（如CNN、ViT）通常作为“黑盒”运行，缺乏可解释性，难以理解其内部决策逻辑；而洪水具有几何结构特征，适合利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）捕捉其全局结构。

Method: 使用SEN12-FLOOD数据集，系统评估了拓扑数据分析（TDA）在洪水检测中的应用，并将提取的拓扑特征整合到神经网络中。

Result: 拓扑描述符能够有效地捕捉洪水图像中的结构化特征，并在与神经网络结合后显著提升了洪水检测的性能和系统的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 拓扑描述符能独立提供洪水信号，并有效作为神经网络的补充，从而实现更加稳健且具有可解释性的洪水检测系统。

Abstract: Floods frequently impact regions around the world. Rapid and accurate flood detection is crucial for emergency response and timely mitigation of human and economic loss. The expanding availability of satellite data and advances in artificial intelligence have enhanced monitoring of environmental hazards, but many flood events remain challenging to detect because cloud cover obscures optical satellite imagery. Rambour et al. introduced the SEN12-FLOOD dataset and extracted per-image features using a ResNet-50 convolutional neural network backbone, then fed these features into a gated recurrent unit network to show that temporal information can substantially improve accuracy compared to single-image baselines. More recently, Chamatidis et al. showed that a vision transformer can achieve strong performance with popular convolutional architectures. However, these models typically function as opaque black boxes, making it difficult to interpret their decision boundaries, learned features, and internal reasoning, especially in safety-critical domains like remote sensing. In contrast, topological data analysis (TDA) provides a mathematically grounded framework for capturing global structural features of data. TDA has emerged as a powerful tool for analyzing complex imagery, especially imagery with geometrically interpretable structures, of which floods are a prime candidate. In this work, we systematically evaluate topological descriptors for flood detection using the open-source SEN12-FLOOD dataset. By extracting topological features from each image and incorporating them into neural networks, we demonstrate that topological descriptors carry meaningful flood signals independently and complement existing networks to yield more robust and interpretable flood detection systems.

</details>


### [234] [A General Framework for Learning Algebraic Properties from Cayley Graphs using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26212)
*Tal Weissblat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于GNN的通用框架，证明了可以仅通过有限群的凯莱图表示来学习并识别其阿贝尔性、幂零性和可解性等代数属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在以往利用GNN预测有限群可解性的基础上，进一步探索利用图表示学习（Graph Representation Learning）从凯莱图中直接提取更为广泛的代数结构信息的可行性。

Method: 提出了一种通用的、与具体属性无关的图神经网络（GNN）框架，通过输入有限群的凯莱图（Cayley graphs）进行训练，以学习并区分群的不同代数特性（阿贝尔性、幂零性、可解性）。

Result: 实验表明，该GNN框架能够成功学习并区分不同群族中的多种代数属性，证明了凯莱图包含足以提取代数结构信息的特征。

Conclusion: 研究证实，有限群的代数结构（如阿贝尔性、幂零性、可解性）确实编码在凯莱图中，且图神经网络（GNN）能够有效地从这些图表示中提取上述信息，为利用深度学习研究抽象代数提供了一个通用的概念验证框架。

Abstract: A Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for predicting the solvability of finite groups from their Cayley graph representations was introduced in [1]. In the present work, we generalize this approach and develop a property-independent framework for learning algebraic properties of finite groups directly from Cayley graphs. As representative case studies, we consider abelianity, nilpotency, and solvability. Using a common GNN architecture and training pipeline, we investigate the extent to which algebraic structure can be recovered from graph-based representations alone. Results on a collection of finite groups drawn from several families demonstrate that the framework successfully learns and distinguishes multiple algebraic properties from their associated Cayley graphs. These findings suggest that substantial algebraic information is encoded in graph representations and can be extracted through GNNs. More broadly, the proposed framework provides a proof of concept for applying graph representation learning to the study of algebraic properties of finite groups.

</details>


### [235] [SOLAR: AI-Powered Speed-of-Light Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26383)
*Qijing Huang,Sana Damani,Zhifan Ye,Athinagoras Skiadopoulos,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Jason Clemons,Sahil Modi,Jingquan Wang,Aditya Kane,Edward C Lin,Humphrey Shi,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLAR 是一个自动化的深度学习“光速”(SOL) 分析框架，通过 LLM 和分析驱动的编译流程，从 PyTorch 和 JAX 代码自动推导模型在特定硬件上的理论最小运行时间，帮助开发者识别性能优化空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习落地中，开发者难以量化模型在特定硬件上的理论最小执行时间（Speed-of-Light, SOL），现有 SOL 分析手段繁琐且容易出错，导致难以评估优化潜力和硬件性能上限。

Method: 提出 SOLAR 框架，结合生成式 AI (LLM) 与确定性分析：1. LLM 前端将模型代码转换为可执行的 Affine Loop IR；2. 通过输出验证确保 IR 正确性；3. 将 IR 提升为 einsum 图；4. 分析后端评估未融合、已融合及缓存感知下的理论运行下限。

Result: SOLAR 在多种基准测试（如 KernelBench、JAX/Flax 模型及机器人负载）中表现稳健，具备广泛的算子与语言覆盖能力。其生成的 SOL 边界在验证中未出现违规情况，并支持多保真度分析，能有效识别性能瓶颈、优化机会及指导硬件配置。

Conclusion: SOLAR 成功实现了对于深度学习模型“光速”(SOL) 执行时间的自动化与高精度估算。该框架填补了模型开发与硬件性能边界之间的鸿沟，为性能分析、架构优化及硬件选型提供了量化依据。

Abstract: How fast could a deep-learning model run on target hardware, and how far is today's implementation from that limit? These questions are central to software, hardware, and algorithm optimizations. Speed-of-Light (SOL) analysis answers them by computing a workload's theoretical minimum execution time on a given architecture. Yet deriving SOL bounds remains manual, error-prone, and disconnected from rapid model development. To close this gap, we introduce SOLAR, a framework that automatically derives validated SOL bounds from PyTorch and JAX source code. SOLAR leverages both generative and deterministic components in its flow: an LLM frontend translates any source programs into an executable Affine Loop IR, validated by output comparison; a deterministic flow lifts the IR into an einsum graph; and an analytical backend computes unfused, fused, and cache-aware SOL bounds. SOLAR provides comprehensive operator and language coverage, produces validated bounds with zero observed SOL violations, and offers multi-fidelity analysis that tightens bounds and surfaces optimization insights. We evaluate SOLAR across KernelBench, JAX/Flax models, and robotics workloads. These experiments demonstrate four use cases: headroom analysis at multiple fidelity levels, identifying optimization opportunities, cross-platform exploration, and inverse-roofline hardware provisioning.

</details>


### [236] [Fast LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26217)
*Yuntian Gao,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决视觉世界模型在规划时的自回归展开计算开销大及误差累积问题，Fast-LeWM 提出直接通过“动作前缀”并行预测未来潜在状态，实现了更高效、精确的视觉规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LeWM 等视觉世界模型在进行视觉规划时，依赖于反复应用局部的单步潜在转换模型（自回归展开）。这种方式不仅计算成本高，而且随着规划视界（horizon）的增加，会导致潜在状态的预测误差累积，影响规划的精确度。

Method: 提出 Fast-LeWM，将传统的基于“逐步”自回归的潜在状态转换改为“动作前缀”预测。模型将当前潜在状态和候选动作序列进行编码，并行预测执行特定前缀后的未来潜在状态，通过预测动作序列中不同长度的累积效果，避免了显式的中间步骤展开。

Result: 在多个任务中，Fast-LeWM 相比 LeWM 显著降低了规划耗时，提升了平均成功率，并在较长的规划视界内实现了更低的潜在空间预测损失及其增长率。

Conclusion: Fast-LeWM 通过引入基于动作前缀（action-prefix）的预测机制，有效解决了传统自回归展开在规划效率和累积误差方面的瓶颈，提供了一种更高效、稳健的视觉世界模型规划方案。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), including recent LeWorldModel (LeWM), have become a promising foundation for reconstruction-free visual world models. For visual planning, however, LeWM evaluates candidate action sequences by repeatedly applying a local one-step latent transition model. This autoregressive rollout makes planning computationally expensive and exposes the predicted trajectory to accumulated latent errors as the horizon grows. We propose Fast LeWorldModel (Fast-LeWM), a fast latent world model that replaces repeated local rollout with action-prefix prediction. Given the current latent and a candidate action sequence, Fast-LeWM encodes its prefixes and predicts the future latents reached after executing those prefixes in parallel. By making action prefixes the basic prediction unit, Fast-LeWM directly models action effects accumulated to different extents over multiple horizons. This prefix-level supervision forces the model to learn how states continuously evolve under different action prefixes, rather than only fitting one-step state transitions. During planning, the predictor can use the last prefix token from the encoded action sequence to evaluate the corresponding future latent without explicitly rolling through each intermediate imagined state. Across multiple tasks, Fast-LeWM improves average success over LeWM while substantially reducing planning time, achieving lower open-loop latent loss whose growth becomes significantly slower as the rollout horizon increases.

</details>


### [237] [Dataset Usage Inference without Shadow Models or Held-out Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26257)
*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak,Jan Dubiński,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需影子模型或保留数据集的实用DUI框架，通过合成数据采样和混合比例估计，有效解决了大型机器学习模型中的数据使用量量化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据集使用推断方法依赖于不切实际的假设（如需要训练昂贵的影子模型或拥有确定的排除训练集的测试集），导致其无法应用于现代大型模型及真实的数据所有权纠纷场景。

Method: 提出了一种无需影子模型（shadow models）或真实保留数据（held-out data）的DUI框架。该方法通过生成合成的非成员样本（synthetic non-member samples），提取多样化的成员信号（membership signals），并将DUI建模为混合比例估计（mixture proportion estimation）问题。

Result: 在大型图像生成模型上的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地量化数据集的使用比例，并在评估过程中消除了对外部数据的强依赖。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种高效、通用的数据集使用推断（DUI）框架，解决了现有方法在大型模型中无法落地的问题，为数据所有者量化数据贡献提供了可靠的审计工具。

Abstract: How much of my data was used to train a machine learning model? Dataset Usage Inference (DUI) aims to answer this by estimating what fraction of a dataset contributed to a model's training. However, existing DUI methods rely on assumptions that rarely hold in practice: they require training expensive shadow models to imitate the target model, and they assume access to both known training samples and an in-distribution held-out set confirmed to be absent from training. These conditions make current approaches impractical for modern large models and real data ownership disputes. We introduce a practical DUI framework that removes these constraints. Our method requires neither shadow models nor real held-out data. Instead, it generates synthetic non-member samples, extracts diverse membership signals, and casts DUI as a mixture proportion estimation problem to estimate what share of the candidate dataset was used during training. Experiments on large image generative models show that our method reliably quantifies dataset usage, providing a practical tool for data owners to determine how much of their data was used to train a model.

</details>


### [238] [Equivariance and Augmentation for Bayesian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26273)
*Miaowen Dong,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分贝叶斯神经网络中的数据增强，证明了在特定条件下可实现精确等变性，并提出了三种新技术（如轨道扩展）来显著提升数据增强在等变性任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对称性神经网络架构（等变网络）与数据增强之间存在争议。现有研究对等变网络理论分析较多，但缺乏对数据增强训练动态的深入理解。本文旨在探索数据增强在变分推断训练的贝叶斯神经网络中的表现及其等变性潜力。

Method: 1. 在变分推断框架下构建贝叶斯神经网络；2. 将变分分布限制在指数族内，推导实现精确等变性的条件；3. 推导等变性误差边界；4. 提出三种新型对称化技术（包含轨道扩展方法）；5. 通过数值实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 研究导出了实现精确等变性的理论条件与误差界限，实验证明所提出的“轨道扩展”对称化技术在提升模型等变性和整体预测性能方面均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过变分推断训练的贝叶斯神经网络（BNNs）在特定条件下可以达到精确的等变性。提出的“轨道扩展”（orbit expansion）等三个对称化技术能够有效提升数据增强的效果，并在等变性和模型性能上优于传统基线方法。

Abstract: Symmetries are important for many deep learning tasks, ranging from applications in the sciences to medical imaging. However, there is an ongoing debate about whether to impose symmetry constraints on the neural network architecture (yielding equivariant neural networks) or learn them from augmented training data. Although equivariant networks are well-studied theoretically, much less is known about data augmentation, since analyzing augmentation requires control over the training dynamics. Inspired by recent results that show that augmented infinite deep ensembles are exactly equivariant, we study data augmentation for Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) trained with variational inference. We focus on variational distributions in the exponential family and derive conditions under which exact equivariance is reached. We furthermore obtain bounds on the equivariance error and introduce three novel symmetrization techniques which boost the effect of data augmentation in this setting. We conduct extensive numerical experiments which show that one of our symmetrization methods (orbit expansion) outperforms the baseline in both equivariance and overall performance. Our code is available at github.com/dmw1998/augment-BNNs

</details>


### [239] [SSM Adapters via Hankel Reduced-order Modeling: Injection Site Determines Task Suitability in Long-Context Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26290)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HRM适配器，一种基于SSM的高效PEFT方法。通过平衡截断初始化和FFT并行扫描，HRM在长文本序列建模任务中显著超越了主流的LoRA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法（如LoRA）多针对注意力机制优化，在需要序列状态累积的任务中表现不足；此外，MLP模块作为PEFT注入点是否更具优势也尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出了一种基于状态空间模型（SSM）的残差模块——Hankel降阶模型（HRM）适配器。它通过经验Hankel Grammians的平衡截断进行初始化，并利用系统矩阵的平移不变性实现基于FFT的精确并行扫描，具备与LoRA相当的计算效率。

Result: 在Mistral-7B模型上进行同参数评估，HRM在LongBench任务中显著优于LoRA（如QuALITY准确率提升34.8%，QMSum ROUGE-1提升71.6%），并在合成状态追踪及字符级语言建模任务中展现出一致的优越性。

Conclusion: HRM适配器为长上下文序列建模提供了一种比低秩适应（LoRA）更高效、性能更强的架构替代方案，特别是在需要序列化状态累积的任务中。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) typically targets attention projectors, its efficacy for tasks requiring sequential state accumulation remains under-explored. We examine if PEFT for such tasks can benefit from state space model (SSMs) adapters, and if MLP blocks are better injection sites. We introduce Hankel Reduced order Model (HRM) adapter, an SSM-based residual module initialized via Balanced Truncation of empirical Hankel Grammians. By leveraging the time-invariance of the system matrix $\bar{A}$, HRM enables an exact FFT-based parallel scan, achieving computational parity with LoRA across all context lengths. In iso-parametric evaluations on Mistral-7B (8.4M trainable parameters), HRM outperforms LoRA variants on LongBench tasks, including QuALITY (+34.8\% relative accuracy) and QMSum (+71.6\% relative ROUGE-1). HRM further demonstrates consistent superiority across 18 configurations of synthetic state-tracking (DFA, Parity) and character-level language modeling (enwik8). Gate analysis reveals that HRM adapters effectively learn to modulate recurrence, providing a robust architectural alternative to low-rank adaptation for long-context sequence modeling.

</details>


### [240] [The Red Queen Gödel Machine: Co-Evolving Agents and Their Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26294)
*Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanović,William F. Shen,Daniel Burkhardt,Meghdad Kurmanji,Nurbek Tastan,Lorenzo Sani,Niccolò Alberto Elia Venanzi,Ambroise Odonnat,Zeyu Cao,Bill Marino,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有智能体递归自改进依赖静态评估的局限，提出了“红皇后歌德尔机” (RQGM)。该框架通过允许评估标准随智能体进化（非平稳效用），实现了更高效、更具对抗性的任务性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进智能体架构多依赖固定静态的基准或验证器，忽略了进化过程中环境（评估标准）应随智能体进步而动态调整的本质，导致难以应对非平稳的进化需求。

Method: 提出“红皇后歌德尔机”（RQGM），采用基于 epoch 的控制策略：在 epoch 内保持固定评估准则以确保改进担保，在 epoch 边界更新效用函数，支持评估者与被评估者协同进化及对抗性训练。

Result: 在代码任务上，RQGM 较现有 SOTA 减少了 1.35x-1.72x 的 token 消耗；在科学论文写作与奥数证明任务中，协同进化的智能体接受率提升 1.78x-1.86x，评分准确率提升 9%，并成功通过对抗性目标纠正了 AI 评阅程序的偏见。

Conclusion: RQGM 框架证明递归自改进能够在非平稳目标（即随时间进化的评估标准）下高效运作，通过引入对抗性与动态评估，不仅提升了任务表现，还优化了计算资源的使用效率（更少的 token），并有效缓解了 AI 评估中的倾向性偏见。

Abstract: Self-improving agents are state-of-the-art (SOTA) on agentic coding benchmarks and have recently been extended to general domains. However, their search methods generally assume a stationary evaluation criterion: a fixed verifier, benchmark, or labeled dataset that remains valid as the agent improves. This ignores a central feature of evolution: species adapt as their environments change with them. We aim to bring the same principle to recursive self-improvement, making evaluation part of the improvement loop and opening search to evolving evaluators, adversarial objectives, and dynamic utilities that may surpass static benchmarks. We introduce the Red Queen Godel Machine (RQGM), an evolutionary framework for recursive self-improvement under non-stationary utilities. The RQGM makes this possible through controlled utility evolution: search is organized into epochs with a fixed within-epoch evaluation criterion, while the utility can be updated at epoch boundaries, so self-improvement guarantees hold per epoch as the objective evolves across them. We begin by showing that even on verifiable coding tasks, the RQGM improves test pass rate over the prior SOTA by adding a complementary agent-as-a-judge code-review signal. This signal is cheaper and the RQGM uses 1.35x-1.72x fewer tokens. We then turn to scientific paper writing and reviewing, and Olympiad-level proof writing and grading, where the RQGM improves performance over prior self-improving agents: co-evolved writers reach 1.78x-1.86x higher acceptance rates under a diverse agent-as-a-judge panel, while co-evolved graders reach 9% higher ground-truth accuracy. In paper reviewing, the strongest baseline reviewer over-accepts AI-generated papers at up to 1.91x the human rate. The RQGM corrects this by introducing an adversarial objective that discovers reviewers equally stringent on AI and human work.

</details>


### [241] [High-Probability PL-SGD with Markovian Noise: Optimal Mixing and Tail Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26316)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Aprameyo Chakrabartty,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究了马尔可夫链驱动下的随机梯度下降（SGD）在PL条件下的收敛性。通过滞后分块和裁剪算法，解决了轻尾假设下收敛速度对混合时间依赖的理论缺口，并给出了重尾分布下的最优收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中，在马尔可夫链驱动下，SGD的高概率收敛界在混合时间依赖上存在 $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$ 与期望界 $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$ 之间的差距，且在重尾噪声存在时的处理方法尚不成熟。本文旨在弥合这一理论差距并建立最优性边界。

Method: 1. 针对轻尾场景，利用“滞后分块”（lag-blocking）技术改进了高概率收敛界；2. 针对重尾场景，设计了一种利用所有样本的裁剪分块（clipped block）算法，并结合马尔可夫链分析；3. 通过构造二次函数和粘性马尔可夫链的归约，建立了相应的下界证明。

Result: 1. 在轻尾环境下，证明了该方法在高概率下达到最优的 $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$ 收敛速度；2. 在重尾环境下（$p$-阶矩条件），证明了该算法达到 $\widetilde{O}(σ_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$ 的高概率误差界，并通过下界证明验证了其最优性。

Conclusion: 本文为在马尔可夫链驱动下的PL条件一阶优化提供了紧致的理论界，明确了轻尾情况下与混合时间（Mixing Time）的线性依赖关系，并推导了重尾情况下的最优收敛速率，实现了理论上的最优性。

Abstract: We study first-order methods for smooth objectives satisfying the Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL) condition when gradient samples are generated by an exogenous Markov chain. In the light-tailed setting, prior uniform-in-time high-probability bounds for ordinary Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) under a standard growth envelope scale as $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$, leaving a gap with the $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$ expectation bounds. We close this gap using a lag-blocking argument to establish a uniform high-probability guarantee with a leading stochastic term of $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$ under geometric mixing. We prove this linear dependence on the mixing time is optimal via a matching $Ω(σ^2 t_{mix}/k)$ lower bound on a quadratic objective driven by a persistent two-state chain.
  We then extend this framework to heavy-tailed Markovian gradients satisfying a stationary finite-$p$-moment condition, $p \in (1,2]$. We design an all-samples clipped block method that uses every Markov transition while mitigating Markovian bias. Under a transition budget $T$, this algorithm achieves a high-probability stochastic error of $\widetilde{O}(σ_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$. We establish a matching lower bound by reducing PL optimization to heavy-tailed mean estimation for a sticky Markov chain. Ultimately, this work tightly characterizes the optimal polynomial dependence on mixing time for light-tailed PL-SGD, and the optimal heavy-tail exponent and effective-sample-size dependence in the robust regime.

</details>


### [242] [EVOM: Agentic Meta-Evolution of Actor-Critic Architectures for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26327)
*Boyun Zhang,Chao Wang,Kai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVOM 是一种自动发现 Actor-Critic 强化学习架构的元进化框架，通过 LLM 设计代理与双层优化过程，实现了优于人工设计和现有 SOTA 方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Actor-Critic 强化学习架构依赖手动设计，而自动神经架构搜索（NAS）面临评估成本高昂和搜索空间开放的挑战。需要一种更高效的自动化方法来发现高性能架构。

Method: 提出名为 EVOM 的元进化框架，采用双层优化策略：内层使用低保真度 PPO 训练模型权重，外层利用基于 LLM 的设计代理（Design Agent）进行架构程序的迭代进化，实现架构设计与策略执行的解耦。

Result: EVOM 在 Ant-v4 和 HalfCheetah-v4 环境中表现优于手动设定的基线模型、LLM 引导的随机搜索以及最前沿的 MLES 方法；消融实验证实了元进化循环和 LLM 设计代理对性能提升的核心作用。

Conclusion: EVOM 通过将 LLM 设计代理与元进化框架结合，为自动化设计高性能 Actor-Critic 架构提供了一种有效且优越的方案。实验证明，该框架在策略性能和计算效率上均优于现有的手动设计及 LLM 引导的搜索方法。

Abstract: In actor-critic reinforcement learning, network architectures are typically manually designed. Automating this design is challenging because each candidate must be trained before evaluation, and the design space is open-ended. To address these challenges, we introduce EVOM, an agentic meta-evolution framework for discovering high-performance actor-critic architectures. We frame architecture search as a bi-level optimization: an inner loop trains weights via the low-fidelity proximal policy optimization (PPO), while an outer loop drives meta-evolution by iteratively refining architecture programs. Crucially, this outer loop is powered by an LLM-based design agent that operates purely as an architecture designer, completely decoupled from policy execution and environment control. Experiments reveal that EVOM outperforms the manually designed baseline, an LLM-guided random search, and the state-of-the-art LLM-guided programmatic policy search method MLES, delivering superior performance on Ant-v4 and HalfCheetah-v4. Ablation studies validate that both the meta-evolution loop and the LLM Design Agent are indispensable for final performance.

</details>


### [243] [Mesh-RL: Coupled subgrid reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26333)
*Behnam Gheshlaghi,Bahador Rashidi,Shahin Atakishiyev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mesh-RL通过空间域分解技术（将环境划分为重叠子网格并强制边界一致性），在不改变奖励函数或贝尔曼算子的前提下，高效解决了强化学习在稀疏奖励环境中的值传播缓慢问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模或稀疏奖励环境中，传统的强化学习因时间差分奖励传播缓慢，导致值信息仅能局部扩散，限制了长距离信用分配的效率。

Method: 受有限元方法和域分解理论启发，将环境划分为重叠的子网格，并强制执行边界一致的时间差分更新，实现局部学习与全局值函数传播的协同。

Result: 在Q-learning、SARSA和Dyna-Q实验中，Mesh-RL显著提升了收敛速度、累积奖励和学习稳定性；较高的网格分辨率能有效促进探索，防止过早收敛并加速远程状态的值传播。

Conclusion: Mesh-RL通过引入空间域分解架构，为强化学习提供了一种非模型化、不修改贝尔曼算子的加速方法，特别适用于稀疏奖励环境，有效解决了值函数传播滞后的问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in large or sparse-reward environments suffers from slow temporal-difference reward propagation, as value information spreads only locally across the state space. We propose Mesh-RL, a spatial domain-decomposition framework inspired by the finite element method and domain decomposition theory, which partitions the environment into overlapping subgrids and enforces boundary-consistent temporal-difference updates. Such an approach enables localized learning while ensuring globally coherent value propagation. Unlike hierarchical or model-based approaches, Mesh-RL accelerates long-range credit assignment without modifying the reward function, Bellman operator, or introducing explicit planning mechanisms. We evaluate Mesh-RL on hazard-dense grid-world environments with varying geometries and mesh resolutions. Across Q-learning, SARSA, and Dyna-Q, Mesh-RL consistently improves convergence speed, cumulative reward, and learning stability. Higher mesh resolutions sustain exploration, prevent premature convergence, and substantially accelerate value propagation to distant states. While Dyna-Q already benefits from internal planning, it still achieves additional gains under structured decomposition. Overall, Mesh-RL introduces a principled spatial domain-decomposition mechanism for accelerating temporal-difference learning. Our framework bridges finite element method-inspired boundary-consistency techniques from scientific computing with reinforcement learning to improve sample efficiency in sparse-reward environments. We will release source code of the study.

</details>


### [244] [EMA-FS: Accelerating GBDT Training via Gain-Informed Feature Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26337)
*Yan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于指数移动平均（EMA）的 GBDT 特征筛选算法（EMA-FS），通过优先保留高分裂增益的特征来优化直方图构建过程，从而在显著提升训练速度的同时保持甚至提高预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBDT 训练过程中直方图构建耗时占比过高，且现有的随机特征子采样忽略了特征的预测价值，导致训练效率与模型性能之间的权衡（trade-off）不理想。

Method: 提出基于指数移动平均（EMA）的特征筛选方法（EMA-FS），通过维护各特征在跨迭代中的分裂增益，仅对高增益特征进行直方图构建；进一步扩展为随机化版本（S-EMA-FS），实现确定性筛选与随机筛选的动态统一。

Result: 在多个数据集上，EMA-FS 实现了最高 2.61 倍的加速，且在减少特征比例的情况下仍能保持甚至提升指标（如 AUC 提升 0.11 点）；该方法通过少量 C++ 代码实现了与 LightGBM 的高度兼容。

Conclusion: EMA-FS 和 S-EMA-FS 是有效的 GBDT 训练加速方案，能在保持模型精度的同时，通过动态筛选高增益特征显著降低直方图构建的时间开销，且具有高度的兼容性和实现简洁性。

Abstract: Gradient Boosted Decision Trees (GBDT), exemplified by LightGBM, spend a dominant fraction of training time -- typically 65-70% -- constructing per-feature histograms. Existing approaches such as random feature subsampling (feature_fraction) discard features without regard for their predictive utility. We propose EMA-based Feature Screening (EMA-FS), an algorithm-level optimization that maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) of per-feature split gains across boosting iterations and, after a short warmup, restricts histogram construction to the top-K features ranked by historical gain. Unlike random subsampling, EMA-FS is informed: it retains high-gain features while screening out low-gain ones. Operating at the per-tree level, it preserves full compatibility with LightGBM's histogram subtraction trick, requiring no changes to core routines.
  We evaluate EMA-FS on datasets spanning financial fraud detection, advertising click-through prediction, industrial quality control, and synthetic benchmarks, with feature dimensionalities from 29 to 968. On dense, moderate-to-high-dimensional data it achieves significant speedups: 2.61x on a 500-feature synthetic benchmark and 1.45x on the 432-feature IEEE-CIS Fraud dataset at 30% retention. At 70% retention it improves AUC by 0.11 points while delivering a 1.34x speedup. On extremely sparse data (Bosch, >90% missing) it yields no speedup, as LightGBM's sparse bin optimization already bypasses empty values.
  We further introduce Stochastic EMA-FS (S-EMA-FS), which replaces deterministic top-K selection with gain-weighted random sampling controlled by a concentration parameter beta, unifying deterministic EMA-FS (beta -> infinity) and random subsampling (beta = 0) in one framework. Both are implemented in ~120 lines of C++ across all six LightGBM tree learners and are fully backward-compatible.

</details>


### [245] [Does Aurora Encode Atmospheric Structure? Latent Regime Analysis and Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26361)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过可解释性技术分析气象基础模型Aurora，揭示了其在未受显式指导下，通过学习季节周期和大气垂直结构来实现准确天气预测的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习基础模型在模拟大气动力学方面表现出色，但其缺乏可解释性，呈现“黑盒”状态，需要对其内部运作机制进行深入探究。

Method: 采用空间池化主成分分析(PCA)分析内部表征；利用逐层相关性传播(LRP)进行可解释性分析；并进行扰动测试以验证重要特征区域对预测准确性的影响。

Result: 验证了潜在空间主要按季节周期组织；LRP证实模型能识别极端天气（如1987年大风暴）的3D垂直结构特征；扰动测试表明屏蔽模型关注的特征区域会导致预测性能下降3.31倍。

Conclusion: Aurora模型无需显式物理干预即可学习并表征气象连贯性与垂直结构，其潜在空间主要受季节性周期驱动，而非极端天气事件。

Abstract: ML foundation models are able to emulate atmospheric dynamics accurately and efficiently but operate as opaque ``black boxes''. We investigate the internal representations of the Aurora model using spatially pooled PCA and layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP). We find evidence that Aurora's latent space is primarily organized by seasonal cycles, whereas extreme storm events do not form a linearly separable cluster. LRP indicates that the model attends to features consistent with the 3D vertical structure of the Great Storm of 1987. Perturbation tests show masking relevant regions degrades forecasts $3.31\times$ more than random masking. These findings suggest that Aurora learns meteorological coherence and vertical structure without explicit instruction.

</details>


### [246] [At the Edge of Understanding: Sparse Autoencoders Trace The Limits of Transformer Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26396)
*Praneet Suresh,Jack Stanley,Sonia Joseph,Luca Scimeca,Danilo Bzdok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过研究Transformer在面对OOD输入时的内部机制，发现此类输入会诱发模型激活更多错误概念，并据此提出了一种通过校正内部计算过程来提升LLM鲁棒性的微调策略和诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer模型在面对分布外（OOD）数据（如拼写错误、诱导性攻击提示词）时，缺乏显式处理机制，导致模型可靠性和安全性下降，亟需对其进行更深入的机理研究。

Method: 通过系统性实验构建了一个机制框架，用于描绘Transformer模型鲁棒性的边界；通过发现OOD输入会触发模型内部更多谬误概念的激活，量化了提示词的分布偏移程度，并据此提出了一种基于机制的微调策略以增强LLM的鲁棒性。

Result: 研究发现OOD输入会导致模型内部激活更多谬误概念；基于这种机理，提出的防御策略能有效识别分布偏移并提升模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将OOD（分布外）的概念从输入数据扩展至模型的内部计算过程，并在推理时提出了一种新的Transformer诊断工具，这是确保大模型在实际部署中更安全、更可靠的关键步骤。

Abstract: Pre-trained transformers have demonstrated remarkable generalization abilities, at times extending beyond the scope of their training data. Yet, real-world deployments often face unexpected or adversarial data that diverges from training data distributions. Without explicit mechanisms for handling such shifts, model reliability and safety degrade, urging more disciplined study of out-of-distribution (OOD) settings for transformers. By systematic experiments, we present a mechanistic framework for delineating the precise contours of transformer model robustness. We find that OOD inputs, including subtle typos and jailbreak prompts, drive language models to operate on an increased number of fallacious concepts in their internals. We leverage this device to quantify and understand the degree of distributional shift in prompts, enabling a mechanistically grounded fine-tuning strategy to robustify LLMs. Expanding the very notion of OOD from input data to a model's private computational processes, a new transformer diagnostic at inference time is a critical step toward making AI systems safe for deployment across science, business, and government.

</details>


### [247] [Deterministic Pareto-Optimal Policy Synthesis for Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26397)
*Aniruddha Joshi,Niklas Lauffer,Sanjit Seshia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切比雪夫标量化的新型强化学习方法，用于解决多目标决策问题，能够高效学习并提取整个帕累托最优前沿的确定性策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习通过标量化将多个冲突目标合并为单一奖励信号，难以捕捉多目标决策中的完整帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier）。

Method: 引入了一种基于切比雪夫标量化（Chebyshev scalarization）的偏好条件贝尔曼算子（preference-conditioned Bellman operator），通过该算子计算多目标马尔可夫决策过程（MOMDPs）的确定性帕累托最优策略，并提出了一种从收敛的Q值函数中提取策略的方法。

Result: 理论上证明了该算子具有包络属性（enveloping property），其估计值函数构成了真实帕累托前沿的上界，且能够单调收敛到帕累托前沿的覆盖集。实验验证了算法能够有效恢复复杂的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基于切比雪夫标量化的偏好条件贝尔曼算子能够收敛到帕累托前沿的覆盖集，并能从中提取出确定性的帕累托最优策略，为多目标决策问题提供了一种理论严谨且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making often requires balancing multiple conflicting objectives, a challenge that standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) frequently addresses by aggregating rewards into a single scalar signal. While effective for simple tasks, this approach often fails to capture the full spectrum of optimal trade-offs, known as the Pareto frontier. In this paper, we introduce a novel preference-conditioned Bellman operator, motivated from the Chebyshev scalarization, designed to compute deterministic Pareto-optimal policies for Multi-Objective Markov Decision Processes (MOMDPs). We prove that this operator satisfies an enveloping property, where the estimated value functions upper-bound the true Pareto frontier, and demonstrate that it monotonically converges to a coverage set of this frontier. Furthermore, we also show how to extract deterministic policies from these converged Q-estimates. This ensures the agent can recover a policy for any given preference, capturing the entire Pareto-optimal frontier while guaranteeing each synthesized policy remains approximately Pareto-optimal. Experimental results validate that our algorithm successfully recovers complex trade-offs, providing a solution for deterministic Pareto-optimal policy synthesis.

</details>
